===== .htaccess =====
# Preserve legacy SIRAH URLs without keeping root-level wrapper files.
RewriteEngine On

# Page routes
RewriteRule ^absensi_anak\.php$ index.php?page=absensi_anak [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^dashboard\.php$ index.php?page=dashboard [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^input_kehadiran\.php$ index.php?page=input_kehadiran [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^input_murajaah\.php$ index.php?page=input_murajaah [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^input_pr\.php$ index.php?page=input_pr [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^input_rapor\.php$ index.php?page=input_rapor [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^input_tasmi\.php$ index.php?page=input_tasmi [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^input_ziyadah\.php$ index.php?page=input_ziyadah [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^kelola-ustadz\.php$ index.php?page=kelola-ustadz [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^kelola_hak_akses\.php$ index.php?page=kelola_hak_akses [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^kelola_kelas\.php$ index.php?page=kelola_kelas [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^kelola_santri\.php$ index.php?page=kelola_santri [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^login\.php$ index.php?page=login [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^monitoring_anak\.php$ index.php?page=monitoring_anak [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^profil_saya\.php$ index.php?page=profil_saya [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^progres_santri\.php$ index.php?page=progres_santri [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^pr_anak\.php$ index.php?page=pr_anak [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^rapor_santri\.php$ index.php?page=rapor_santri [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^rekap_bulanan\.php$ index.php?page=rekap_bulanan [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^target_santri\.php$ index.php?page=target_santri [QSA,L]

# Existing action URLs now execute their organized API handlers.
RewriteRule ^logout\.php$ api/auth/logout.php [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^proses_login\.php$ api/auth/login.php [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^proses_input_hafalan\.php$ api/hafalan/store.php [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^proses_input_rapor\.php$ api/rapor/store.php [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^proses_tambah_santri\.php$ api/santri/store.php [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^proses_edit_santri\.php$ api/santri/update.php [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^proses_hapus_santri\.php$ api/santri/delete.php [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^proses_target_santri\.php$ api/santri/target.php [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^proses_tambah_ustadz\.php$ api/pengguna/store.php [QSA,L]
RewriteRule ^proses_hapus_ustadz\.php$ api/pengguna/delete.php [QSA,L]

===== api/auth/login.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] !== 'POST') {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$username = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['username']);
$password = $_POST['password'];

$query = "SELECT * FROM users WHERE username = '$username'";
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);

if (mysqli_num_rows($result) === 1) {
    $user = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result);

    if (password_verify($password, $user['password'])) {
        $_SESSION['user_id']      = $user['id'];
        $_SESSION['username']     = $user['username'];
        $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] = $user['nama_lengkap'];
        $_SESSION['role']         = $user['role'];

        header("Location: dashboard.php");
        exit;
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Password salah!";
        header("Location: login.php");
        exit;
    }
} else {
    $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Username tidak ditemukan!";
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}
?>

===== api/auth/logout.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
session_destroy();
header("Location: login.php");
exit;
?>

===== api/hafalan/store.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom `juz` dan `pojok` di tabel `hafalan` jika belum ada
$cek_juz = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM hafalan LIKE 'juz'");
if ($cek_juz && mysqli_num_rows($cek_juz) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `juz` INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `jenis`");
}
$cek_pojok = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM hafalan LIKE 'pojok'");
if ($cek_pojok && mysqli_num_rows($cek_pojok) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `pojok` VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `juz`");
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $id_santri  = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $jenis      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis'] ?? 'ziyadah');
    $juz        = !empty($_POST['juz']) ? (int) $_POST['juz'] : 'NULL';
    $pojok      = !empty($_POST['pojok']) ? "'" . mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['pojok'])) . "'" : 'NULL';
    $surat      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['surat']));
    $ayat_awal  = (int) ($_POST['ayat_awal'] ?? 1);
    $ayat_akhir = (int) ($_POST['ayat_akhir'] ?? 1);
    $predikat   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['predikat']);
    $catatan    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['catatan'] ?? ''));
    $id_ustadz  = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
    $tanggal    = !empty($_POST['tanggal']) ? mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal']) : date('Y-m-d');

    $jumlah_ayat = ($ayat_akhir >= $ayat_awal) ? ($ayat_akhir - $ayat_awal + 1) : 1;

    $sql = "INSERT INTO hafalan (id_santri, tanggal, jenis, juz, pojok, surat, ayat_awal, ayat_akhir, jumlah_ayat, predikat, catatan, id_ustadz) 
            VALUES ($id_santri, '$tanggal', '$jenis', $juz, $pojok, '$surat', $ayat_awal, $ayat_akhir, $jumlah_ayat, '$predikat', '$catatan', $id_ustadz)";

    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data setoran " . ucfirst($jenis) . " berhasil disimpan ($jumlah_ayat Ayat)!";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal menyimpan data: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }

    // Redirect kembali ke halaman input yang sesuai
    $map_halaman = [
        'ziyadah'  => 'input_ziyadah.php',
        'murajaah' => 'input_murajaah.php',
        'tasmi'    => 'input_tasmi.php'
    ];
    $tujuan = $map_halaman[$jenis] ?? 'input_ziyadah.php';
    header("Location: $tujuan");
    exit;
} else {
    header("Location: input_ziyadah.php");
    exit;
}
?>

===== api/pengguna/delete.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'pimpinan', 'ustadz'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if (isset($_GET['id'])) {
    $id = (int) $_GET['id']; 

    $query = "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $id AND role = 'ustadz'";
    
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $query)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "🗑️ Data ustadz/ustadzah berhasil dihapus.";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal menghapus data.";
    }
}

header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
exit;
?>

===== api/pengguna/store.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'pimpinan', 'ustadz'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $nama_lengkap = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['nama_lengkap']);
    $username = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['username']);
    $password = $_POST['password'];
    $role = 'ustadz'; 

    $check = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($check) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Username '$username' sudah digunakan! Silakan gunakan username lain.";
        header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
        exit;
    }

    $password_hash = password_hash($password, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);

    $query = "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) 
              VALUES ('$username', '$password_hash', '$nama_lengkap', '$role')";

    if (mysqli_query($conn, $query)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "✅ Data ustadz/ustadzah berhasil ditambahkan!";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Terjadi kesalahan saat menyimpan data.";
    }

    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}
?>

===== api/rapor/store.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Pastikan tabel rapor_tahfidz & rapor_tahfidz_detail beserta kolom tambahannya tersedia otomatis
mysqli_query($conn, "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `rapor_tahfidz` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `id_santri` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `kelas` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `judul_materi` varchar(100) DEFAULT 'Materi Tahfidz',
  `semester` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `tahun_ajaran` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `juz` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `id_ustadz` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4");

mysqli_query($conn, "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `rapor_tahfidz_detail` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `id_rapor` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `urutan` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `nama_materi` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `nilai` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4");

// Tambahkan kolom ekstra khusus Rapor Juz 30 jika belum ada
$extra_cols = [
    "nilai_tahsin" => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "naik_juz"     => "VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT '-'",
    "remedi_juz"   => "VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT '-'",
    "ujian_juz_30" => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "ujian_juz_1"  => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "ujian_juz_2"  => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "ujian_juz_3"  => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "ujian_juz_4"  => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "akhlak"       => "VARCHAR(5) DEFAULT 'A'",
    "kerajinan"    => "VARCHAR(5) DEFAULT 'B'",
    "kebersihan"   => "VARCHAR(5) DEFAULT 'A'",
    "kerapian"     => "VARCHAR(5) DEFAULT 'A'",
    "sakit"        => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "izin"         => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "alfa"         => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "catatan_ortu" => "TEXT DEFAULT NULL",
    "tempat_cetak" => "VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Jalikur Patanangan'",
    "tanggal_cetak"=> "VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT NULL",
    "wali_kelas"   => "VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL",
    "kepala_stq"   => "VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Ust. Aipda Syaiful Hendra'"
];

foreach ($extra_cols as $col_name => $col_def) {
    $cek_c = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM rapor_tahfidz LIKE '$col_name'");
    if ($cek_c && mysqli_num_rows($cek_c) === 0) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE rapor_tahfidz ADD COLUMN `$col_name` $col_def");
    }
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $id_santri    = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $kelas        = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['kelas']));
    $judul_materi = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['judul_materi']));
    $semester     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['semester']));
    $tahun_ajaran = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['tahun_ajaran']));
    $juz          = (int) $_POST['juz'];
    $id_ustadz    = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

    // Data tambahan (terutama untuk Rapor Juz 30)
    $nilai_tahsin = ($_POST['nilai_tahsin'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['nilai_tahsin'] : 'NULL';
    $naik_juz     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['naik_juz'] ?? '-'));
    $remedi_juz   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['remedi_juz'] ?? '-'));
    $ujian_30     = ($_POST['ujian_juz_30'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['ujian_juz_30'] : 'NULL';
    $ujian_1      = ($_POST['ujian_juz_1'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['ujian_juz_1'] : 'NULL';
    $ujian_2      = ($_POST['ujian_juz_2'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['ujian_juz_2'] : 'NULL';
    $ujian_3      = ($_POST['ujian_juz_3'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['ujian_juz_3'] : 'NULL';
    $ujian_4      = ($_POST['ujian_juz_4'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['ujian_juz_4'] : 'NULL';
    $akhlak       = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['akhlak'] ?? 'A');
    $kerajinan    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['kerajinan'] ?? 'B');
    $kebersihan   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['kebersihan'] ?? 'A');
    $kerapian     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['kerapian'] ?? 'A');
    $sakit        = ($_POST['sakit'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['sakit'] : 'NULL';
    $izin         = ($_POST['izin'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['izin'] : 'NULL';
    $alfa         = ($_POST['alfa'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['alfa'] : 'NULL';
    $catatan_ortu = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['catatan_ortu'] ?? ''));
    $tempat_cetak = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['tempat_cetak'] ?? 'Jalikur Patanangan'));
    $tanggal_cetak= mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['tanggal_cetak'] ?? date('d F Y')));
    $wali_kelas   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['wali_kelas'] ?? ''));
    $kepala_stq   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['kepala_stq'] ?? 'Ust. Aipda Syaiful Hendra'));

    // Cek apakah Rapor Header untuk santri & juz ini sudah ada
    $cek_query = "SELECT id FROM rapor_tahfidz WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND juz = $juz AND semester = '$semester' AND tahun_ajaran = '$tahun_ajaran'";
    $cek_result = mysqli_query($conn, $cek_query);

    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_result) > 0) {
        $row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_result);
        $id_rapor = (int) $row['id'];
        $sql_upd = "UPDATE rapor_tahfidz SET 
                        kelas = '$kelas',
                        judul_materi = '$judul_materi',
                        id_ustadz = $id_ustadz,
                        nilai_tahsin = $nilai_tahsin,
                        naik_juz = '$naik_juz',
                        remedi_juz = '$remedi_juz',
                        ujian_juz_30 = $ujian_30,
                        ujian_juz_1 = $ujian_1,
                        ujian_juz_2 = $ujian_2,
                        ujian_juz_3 = $ujian_3,
                        ujian_juz_4 = $ujian_4,
                        akhlak = '$akhlak',
                        kerajinan = '$kerajinan',
                        kebersihan = '$kebersihan',
                        kerapian = '$kerapian',
                        sakit = $sakit,
                        izin = $izin,
                        alfa = $alfa,
                        catatan_ortu = '$catatan_ortu',
                        tempat_cetak = '$tempat_cetak',
                        tanggal_cetak = '$tanggal_cetak',
                        wali_kelas = '$wali_kelas',
                        kepala_stq = '$kepala_stq'
                    WHERE id = $id_rapor";
        mysqli_query($conn, $sql_upd);
        mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM rapor_tahfidz_detail WHERE id_rapor = $id_rapor");
    } else {
        $sql_ins = "INSERT INTO rapor_tahfidz (
                        id_santri, kelas, judul_materi, semester, tahun_ajaran, juz, id_ustadz,
                        nilai_tahsin, naik_juz, remedi_juz, ujian_juz_30, ujian_juz_1, ujian_juz_2, ujian_juz_3, ujian_juz_4,
                        akhlak, kerajinan, kebersihan, kerapian, sakit, izin, alfa, catatan_ortu, tempat_cetak, tanggal_cetak, wali_kelas, kepala_stq
                    ) VALUES (
                        $id_santri, '$kelas', '$judul_materi', '$semester', '$tahun_ajaran', $juz, $id_ustadz,
                        $nilai_tahsin, '$naik_juz', '$remedi_juz', $ujian_30, $ujian_1, $ujian_2, $ujian_3, $ujian_4,
                        '$akhlak', '$kerajinan', '$kebersihan', '$kerapian', $sakit, $izin, $alfa, '$catatan_ortu', '$tempat_cetak', '$tanggal_cetak', '$wali_kelas', '$kepala_stq'
                    )";
        mysqli_query($conn, $sql_ins);
        $id_rapor = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
    }

    // Simpan Detail Materi & Nilai
    $urutan = 1;
    if (isset($_POST['materi']) && isset($_POST['nilai'])) {
        $materi_arr = $_POST['materi'];
        $nilai_arr  = $_POST['nilai'];

        for ($i = 0; $i < count($materi_arr); $i++) {
            $nama_materi = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($materi_arr[$i]));
            $nilai_raw   = trim((string)($nilai_arr[$i] ?? ''));
            $nilai       = ($nilai_raw !== '') ? max(0, min(100, (int)$nilai_raw)) : 'NULL';
            
            if (!empty($nama_materi)) {
                mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO rapor_tahfidz_detail (id_rapor, urutan, nama_materi, nilai) 
                                     VALUES ($id_rapor, $urutan, '$nama_materi', $nilai)");
                $urutan++;
            }
        }
    }

    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data Leger Rapor Juz $juz berhasil disimpan (" . ($urutan - 1) . " baris materi)!";
    header("Location: input_rapor.php?id_santri=$id_santri&juz=$juz&semester=" . urlencode($semester) . "&tahun_ajaran=" . urlencode($tahun_ajaran));
    exit;
}
?>

===== api/santri/delete.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if (isset($_GET['id'])) {
    $id_santri = (int) $_GET['id'];

   
    $query_cari = "SELECT id_user_ortu FROM santri WHERE id = $id_santri";
    $result = mysqli_query($conn, $query_cari);
    $data = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result);
    $id_user_ortu = $data['id_user_ortu'];

 
    if (mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM santri WHERE id = $id_santri")) {
        
    
        if ($id_user_ortu) {
            mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $id_user_ortu AND role = 'ortu'");
        }
        
        $_SESSION['success'] = "🗑️ Data santri dan akun ortu berhasil dihapus.";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal menghapus data santri.";
    }
}

header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
exit;
?>

===== api/santri/store.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// ️ UBAH 1: Ambil ID Ustadz yang sedang login
$id_ustadz = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $nama_santri    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['nama_santri']);
    $nis            = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['nis']);
    $jk             = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $tgl_lahir      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal_lahir']);
    $alamat         = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['alamat']);
    $nama_ortu      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['nama_ortu']);
    $no_hp_ortu     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['no_hp_ortu']);
    $username_ortu  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['username_ortu']);
    $password_ortu  = $_POST['password_ortu'];
    $id_halaqah     = !empty($_POST['id_halaqah']) ? (int) $_POST['id_halaqah'] : "NULL";

    $check = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username_ortu'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($check) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = " Username orang tua '$username_ortu' sudah digunakan!";
        header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
        exit;
    }
    $hash_ortu = password_hash($password_ortu, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);

    $sql_user = "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) 
                 VALUES ('$username_ortu', '$hash_ortu', '$nama_ortu', 'ortu')";
    
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql_user)) {
    
        $id_user_ortu = mysqli_insert_id($conn);

        // ⬅️ UBAH 2: Tambahkan id_ustadz dan id_halaqah di query INSERT
        $sql_santri = "INSERT INTO santri (nama, nis, jenis_kelamin, tanggal_lahir, alamat, nama_ortu, no_hp_ortu, id_user_ortu, id_halaqah, id_ustadz) 
                       VALUES ('$nama_santri', '$nis', '$jk', '$tgl_lahir', '$alamat', '$nama_ortu', '$no_hp_ortu', $id_user_ortu, $id_halaqah, $id_ustadz)";
        
        if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql_santri)) {
            $_SESSION['success'] = "✅ Santri '$nama_santri' dan akun Ortu berhasil ditambahkan!";
        } else {
            $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal simpan data santri: " . mysqli_error($conn);
        }
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal membuat akun ortu: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }

    header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
    exit;
}
?>

===== api/santri/target.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya Ustadz dan Pimpinan
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    // 1. Ambil dan bersihkan data
    $id_santri      = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $target_ayat    = (int) $_POST['target_ayat'];
    $tanggal_mulai  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal_mulai']);
    $keterangan     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['keterangan']);

    // 2. Cek apakah santri ini sudah punya target sebelumnya
    $cek_query = "SELECT id FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
    $cek_result = mysqli_query($conn, $cek_query);

    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_result) > 0) {
        // SUDAH ADA -> Lakukan UPDATE
        $data = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_result);
        $id_target = $data['id'];

        $sql = "UPDATE target_hafalan 
                SET target_ayat_per_hari = $target_ayat, 
                    tanggal_mulai = '$tanggal_mulai', 
                    keterangan = '$keterangan' 
                WHERE id = $id_target";
        
        $pesan = "Target santri berhasil diperbarui!";
    } else {
        // BELUM ADA -> Lakukan INSERT
        $sql = "INSERT INTO target_hafalan (id_santri, tanggal_mulai, target_ayat_per_hari, keterangan) 
                VALUES ($id_santri, '$tanggal_mulai', $target_ayat, '$keterangan')";
        
        $pesan = "Target santri baru berhasil ditetapkan!";
    }

    // 3. Eksekusi Query
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "✅ $pesan";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal menyimpan target: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }

    // 4. Kembali ke halaman target
    header("Location: target_santri.php");
    exit;
} else {
    header("Location: target_santri.php");
    exit;
}
?>

===== api/santri/update.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $id_santri     = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $id_user_ortu  = (int) $_POST['id_user_ortu'];
    $nama_santri   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_santri']));
    $nis           = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nis']));
    $jk            = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $tgl_lahir     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal_lahir']);
    $alamat        = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['alamat']));
    $nama_ortu     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_ortu']));
    $no_hp_ortu    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['no_hp_ortu']));
    $username_ortu = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username_ortu']));
    $password_baru = trim($_POST['password_baru'] ?? '');
    $id_halaqah    = !empty($_POST['id_halaqah']) ? (int) $_POST['id_halaqah'] : "NULL";

    // 1. Cek duplikasi NIS pada santri lain
    $cek_nis = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM santri WHERE nis = '$nis' AND id != $id_santri");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_nis) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "NIS '$nis' sudah terdaftar pada santri lain!";
        header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
        exit;
    }

    // 2. Update atau buat akun user orang tua
    if ($id_user_ortu > 0) {
        $cek_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username_ortu' AND id != $id_user_ortu");
        if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_user) > 0) {
            $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username_ortu' sudah dipakai akun lain!";
            header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
            exit;
        }

        if (!empty($password_baru)) {
            $hash = password_hash($password_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET username = '$username_ortu', nama_lengkap = '$nama_ortu', password = '$hash' WHERE id = $id_user_ortu");
        } else {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET username = '$username_ortu', nama_lengkap = '$nama_ortu' WHERE id = $id_user_ortu");
        }
    } elseif (!empty($username_ortu)) {
        // Jika santri lama belum memiliki akun ortu, buatkan otomatis
        $pass_default = !empty($password_baru) ? $password_baru : 'ortu123';
        $hash = password_hash($pass_default, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        if (mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) VALUES ('$username_ortu', '$hash', '$nama_ortu', 'ortu')")) {
            $id_user_ortu = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
        }
    }

    // 3. Update tabel santri
    $id_ortu_sql = $id_user_ortu > 0 ? $id_user_ortu : "NULL";
    $sql_update = "UPDATE santri SET 
                    nama = '$nama_santri',
                    nis = '$nis',
                    jenis_kelamin = '$jk',
                    tanggal_lahir = '$tgl_lahir',
                    alamat = '$alamat',
                    nama_ortu = '$nama_ortu',
                    no_hp_ortu = '$no_hp_ortu',
                    id_user_ortu = $id_ortu_sql,
                    id_halaqah = $id_halaqah
                   WHERE id = $id_santri";

    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql_update)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data santri '$nama_santri' berhasil diperbarui!";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal memperbarui data: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }

    header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
    exit;
}
?>

===== assets/js/app.js =====
// Shared SIRAH JavaScript for every page.
const routeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('page');
const pathPage = window.location.pathname.split('/').pop().replace(/\.php$/, '');
const currentPage = routeParam || (pathPage === 'index' || !pathPage ? 'dashboard' : pathPage);
if (window.lucide) window.lucide.createIcons();
function readPageData() {
    const el = document.getElementById('sirah-page-data');
    if (!el) return {};
    try { return JSON.parse(el.textContent); } catch (error) { console.error('Data halaman tidak valid.', error); return {}; }
}
const pageInitializers = {
    'kelola_santri': () => {
        // Buka / Tutup Form Tambah Santri
                function toggleFormSantri() {
                    const form = document.getElementById('formTambahContainer');
                    form.classList.toggle('hidden');
                    if (!form.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                        document.getElementById('inputNamaSantri').focus();
                    }
                }
        
                // Otomatis sarankan username ortu dari kata pertama nama santri
                function generateUsernameOrtu(nama) {
                    const inputUser = document.getElementById('inputUsernameOrtu');
                    const bersih = nama.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, '');
                    const kataPertama = bersih.split(/\s+/)[0] || '';
                    inputUser.value = kataPertama ? 'ortu_' + kataPertama : '';
                }
        
                // Filter Live Search & Jenis Kelamin
                function filterTabelSantri() {
                    const keyword = document.getElementById('cariSantri').value.toLowerCase();
                    const jk = document.getElementById('filterJK').value;
                    const baris = document.querySelectorAll('.baris-santri');
                    let tampil = 0;
        
                    baris.forEach((tr) => {
                        const dataSearch = tr.getAttribute('data-search');
                        const dataJk = tr.getAttribute('data-jk');
                        
                        const cocokKata = dataSearch.includes(keyword);
                        const cocokJk = (jk === 'ALL' || dataJk === jk);
        
                        if (cocokKata && cocokJk) {
                            tr.style.display = '';
                            tampil++;
                            tr.querySelector('.nomor-urut').innerText = tampil;
                        } else {
                            tr.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    });
        
                    document.getElementById('jumlahTampil').innerText = tampil;
                }
        
                function bukaModalEdit(data) {
                    document.getElementById('edit_id_santri').value = data.id;
                    document.getElementById('edit_id_user_ortu').value = data.id_user_ortu || 0;
                    document.getElementById('edit_nama').value = data.nama || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_nis').value = data.nis || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_jk').value = data.jenis_kelamin || 'L';
                    document.getElementById('edit_tgl_lahir').value = data.tanggal_lahir || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_alamat').value = data.alamat || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_nama_ortu').value = data.nama_ortu || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_no_hp').value = data.no_hp_ortu || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_username_ortu').value = data.username_ortu || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_id_halaqah').value = data.id_halaqah || '';
                    
                    document.getElementById('modalEditSantri').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        
                function tutupModalEdit() {
                    document.getElementById('modalEditSantri').classList.add('hidden');
                }
        
        window.toggleFormSantri = toggleFormSantri;
        window.generateUsernameOrtu = generateUsernameOrtu;
        window.filterTabelSantri = filterTabelSantri;
        window.bukaModalEdit = bukaModalEdit;
        window.tutupModalEdit = tutupModalEdit;
    },
    'pr_anak': () => {
        let statusFilterAktif = 'ALL';
        
                function setFilterPR(status, btn) {
                    statusFilterAktif = status;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-filter').forEach(b => {
                        b.className = 'tab-filter bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition';
                    });
                    btn.className = 'tab-filter bg-slate-900 text-white text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition';
                    terapkanFilterPR();
                }
        
                function terapkanFilterPR() {
                    const keyword = document.getElementById('cariPR').value.toLowerCase();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.kartu-pr').forEach(card => {
                        const cocokStatus = (statusFilterAktif === 'ALL' || card.dataset.status === statusFilterAktif);
                        const cocokKata   = card.dataset.search.includes(keyword);
                        card.style.display = (cocokStatus && cocokKata) ? '' : 'none';
                    });
                }
        
        window.setFilterPR = setFilterPR;
        window.terapkanFilterPR = terapkanFilterPR;
    },
    'rapor_santri': () => {
        
    },
    'input_tasmi': () => {
        function isiOtomatisMateriTasmi(juz) {
                    const inp = document.getElementById('inputMateriTasmi');
                    if (juz == '30') {
                        inp.value = "Juz 30 (An-Naba' s/d An-Nas)";
                    } else if (juz) {
                        inp.value = "Al-Qur'an Juz " + juz + " (20 Pojok)";
                    }
                }
        
                function filterRiwayatTasmi() {
                    const q = document.getElementById('cariTasmi').value.toLowerCase();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.baris-tasmi').forEach(tr => {
                        tr.style.display = tr.dataset.search.includes(q) ? '' : 'none';
                    });
                }
        
                const toastEl = document.getElementById('floatingPanduan');
                if (localStorage.getItem('hide_panduan_tasmi') !== '1') toastEl.classList.remove('hidden');
                function tutupNotifPanduan() { toastEl.classList.add('hidden'); localStorage.setItem('hide_panduan_tasmi', '1'); }
                function tampilkanNotifPanduan() { toastEl.classList.remove('hidden'); localStorage.removeItem('hide_panduan_tasmi'); }
        
        window.isiOtomatisMateriTasmi = isiOtomatisMateriTasmi;
        window.filterRiwayatTasmi = filterRiwayatTasmi;
        window.tutupNotifPanduan = tutupNotifPanduan;
        window.tampilkanNotifPanduan = tampilkanNotifPanduan;
    },
    'target_santri': () => {
        const mapPresetAyat = {
                    "Juz 30 (Juz Amma)": 564, "Juz 29 (Tabarak)": 431, "Juz 1": 148,
                    "QS. Al-Baqarah": 286, "QS. Al-Kahf": 110, "QS. Yasin": 83,
                    "QS. Ar-Rahman": 78, "QS. Al-Waqi'ah": 96, "QS. Al-Mulk": 30,
                    "QS. An-Naba'": 40, "QS. An-Nazi'at": 46, "QS. 'Abasa": 42
                };
        
                function cekPresetAyat(val) {
                    if (mapPresetAyat[val]) {
                        document.getElementById('total_ayat').value = mapPresetAyat[val];
                        hitungEstimasi();
                    }
                }
        
                function hitungEstimasi() {
                    const total = parseInt(document.getElementById('total_ayat').value, 10) || 0;
                    const speed = parseInt(document.getElementById('target_ayat').value, 10) || 0;
                    const tglMulai = document.getElementById('tgl_mulai').value;
        
                    if (total > 0 && speed > 0 && tglMulai) {
                        const hari = Math.ceil(total / speed);
                        const p = tglMulai.split('-');
                        const tglSelesai = new Date(parseInt(p[0], 10), parseInt(p[1], 10) - 1, parseInt(p[2], 10));
                        tglSelesai.setDate(tglSelesai.getDate() + hari);
                        document.getElementById('preview_hari').innerText = hari + " Hari";
                        document.getElementById('preview_tanggal').innerText = tglSelesai.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
                    } else {
                        document.getElementById('preview_hari').innerText = "- Hari";
                        document.getElementById('preview_tanggal').innerText = "Belum dihitung";
                    }
                }
        
                function muatKeForm(data) {
                    document.getElementById('id_santri').value = String(data.id_santri);
                    document.getElementById('target_surat').value = data.target_surat || '';
                    document.getElementById('total_ayat').value = data.total_ayat_target || '';
                    document.getElementById('target_ayat').value = data.target_ayat_per_hari || '';
                    document.getElementById('tgl_mulai').value = data.tanggal_mulai || '';
                    document.getElementById('keterangan').value = data.keterangan || '';
                    hitungEstimasi();
                    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
                }
        
                ['total_ayat', 'target_ayat', 'tgl_mulai', 'target_surat'].forEach(function (id) {
                    var el = document.getElementById(id);
                    ['input', 'change', 'keyup'].forEach(function (ev) {
                        el.addEventListener(ev, function () {
                            if (id === 'target_surat') cekPresetAyat(el.value); else hitungEstimasi();
                        });
                    });
                });
                hitungEstimasi();
        
                
        window.cekPresetAyat = cekPresetAyat;
        window.hitungEstimasi = hitungEstimasi;
        window.muatKeForm = muatKeForm;
    },
    'input_kehadiran': () => {
        
    },
    'profil_saya': () => {
        function togglePass(id) {
                    const el = document.getElementById(id);
                    el.type = el.type === 'password' ? 'text' : 'password';
                }
        
        window.togglePass = togglePass;
    },
    'input_ziyadah': () => {
        // Hitung otomatis rentang jumlah ayat
                function hitungRentangAyat() {
                    const awal = parseInt(document.getElementById('ayatAwal').value) || 0;
                    const akhir = parseInt(document.getElementById('ayatAkhir').value) || 0;
                    const badge = document.getElementById('badgeJumlahAyat');
        
                    if (awal > 0 && akhir >= awal) {
                        const total = akhir - awal + 1;
                        badge.innerText = total + ' Ayat Dihafal';
                        badge.classList.remove('hidden');
                    } else {
                        badge.classList.add('hidden');
                    }
                }
        
                // Info estimasi halaman mushaf ketika Juz & Pojok dipilih
                function updateInfoHalamanMushaf() {
                    const juz = parseInt(document.getElementById('inputJuz').value) || 0;
                    const pojokStr = document.getElementById('inputPojok').value || '';
                    const match = pojokStr.match(/\d+/);
                    const infoEl = document.getElementById('infoHalamanMushaf');
        
                    if (juz > 0 && match) {
                        const p = parseInt(match[0]);
                        if (p >= 1 && p <= 20) {
                            const halMushaf = (juz - 1) * 20 + p + 1;
                            infoEl.innerText = (juz === 1 && p === 1) ? '(Al-Fatihah + Hal 2)' : '(Hal. ' + halMushaf + ' Mushaf)';
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    infoEl.innerText = '';
                }
        
                // Filter pencarian tabel riwayat hari ini
                function filterRiwayatHariIni() {
                    const q = document.getElementById('cariRiwayat').value.toLowerCase();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.baris-riwayat').forEach(tr => {
                        tr.style.display = tr.dataset.search.includes(q) ? '' : 'none';
                    });
                }
        
                // Kontrol Floating Toast Panduan (Bisa Disilang)
                const toastEl = document.getElementById('floatingPanduan');
                if (localStorage.getItem('hide_panduan_ziyadah') !== '1') {
                    toastEl.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
        
                function tutupNotifPanduan() {
                    toastEl.classList.add('hidden');
                    localStorage.setItem('hide_panduan_ziyadah', '1');
                }
        
                function tampilkanNotifPanduan() {
                    toastEl.classList.remove('hidden');
                    localStorage.removeItem('hide_panduan_ziyadah');
                }
        
        window.hitungRentangAyat = hitungRentangAyat;
        window.updateInfoHalamanMushaf = updateInfoHalamanMushaf;
        window.filterRiwayatHariIni = filterRiwayatHariIni;
        window.tutupNotifPanduan = tutupNotifPanduan;
        window.tampilkanNotifPanduan = tampilkanNotifPanduan;
    },
    'kelola_hak_akses': () => {
        
    },
    'absensi_anak': () => {
        function filterLogAbsensi() {
                    const st = document.getElementById('filterStatus').value;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.baris-absensi').forEach(tr => {
                        tr.style.display = (st === 'ALL' || tr.dataset.status === st) ? '' : 'none';
                    });
                }
        
        window.filterLogAbsensi = filterLogAbsensi;
    },

    'kelola-ustadz': () => {
        function toggleFormPengajar() {
                    const form = document.getElementById('formPengajarContainer');
                    form.classList.toggle('hidden');
                    if (!form.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                        document.getElementById('inputNamaPengajar').focus();
                    }
                }
        
                function updatePrefixPengajar() {
                    const jk = document.querySelector('input[name="jenis_kelamin"]:checked').value;
                    const passInput = document.getElementById('inputPasswordPengajar');
                    if (passInput.value === 'ustadz123' || passInput.value === 'ustadzah123') {
                        passInput.value = (jk === 'P') ? 'ustadzah123' : 'ustadz123';
                    }
                    generateUsernamePengajar();
                }
        
                function generateUsernamePengajar() {
                    const nama = document.getElementById('inputNamaPengajar').value;
                    const jk = document.querySelector('input[name="jenis_kelamin"]:checked').value;
                    const prefix = (jk === 'P') ? 'ustadzah_' : 'ustadz_';
        
                    // Hilangkan kata sapaan ustadz/ustadzah dari nama agar tidak dobel di username
                    const bersih = nama.toLowerCase()
                        .replace(/\bustadzah\b/g, '')
                        .replace(/\bustadz\b/g, '')
                        .replace(/\bust\b/g, '')
                        .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, '')
                        .trim();
        
                    const kataPertama = bersih.split(/\s+/)[0] || '';
                    document.getElementById('inputUsernamePengajar').value = kataPertama ? prefix + kataPertama : '';
                }
        
                function filterPengajar() {
                    const keyword = document.getElementById('cariPengajar').value.toLowerCase();
                    const kat = document.getElementById('filterKategori').value;
                    const baris = document.querySelectorAll('.baris-pengajar');
                    let tampil = 0;
        
                    baris.forEach(tr => {
                        const cocokKata = tr.dataset.search.includes(keyword);
                        const cocokKat  = (kat === 'ALL' || tr.dataset.jk === kat);
                        if (cocokKata && cocokKat) {
                            tr.style.display = '';
                            tampil++;
                            tr.querySelector('.nomor-urut').innerText = tampil;
                        } else {
                            tr.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    });
                    document.getElementById('jumlahTampil').innerText = tampil;
                }
        
                function bukaModalEditPengajar(data, jk) {
                    document.getElementById('edit_id_user').value = data.id;
                    document.getElementById('edit_jk_pengajar').value = jk;
                    document.getElementById('edit_nama_pengajar').value = data.nama_lengkap || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_lokal_pengajar').value = data.nama_halaqah || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_username_pengajar').value = data.username || '';
                    document.getElementById('modalEditPengajar').classList.remove('hidden');
                }
        
                function tutupModalEditPengajar() {
                    document.getElementById('modalEditPengajar').classList.add('hidden');
                }
        
        window.toggleFormPengajar = toggleFormPengajar;
        window.updatePrefixPengajar = updatePrefixPengajar;
        window.generateUsernamePengajar = generateUsernamePengajar;
        window.filterPengajar = filterPengajar;
        window.bukaModalEditPengajar = bukaModalEditPengajar;
        window.tutupModalEditPengajar = tutupModalEditPengajar;
    },
    'input_pr': () => {

        window.filterRiwayatPR = filterRiwayatPR;
        window.tutupNotifPanduan = tutupNotifPanduan;

    'kelola_kelas': () => {
        let filterKetAktif = 'ALL';
        
                function filterKategoriLokal(ket, btn) {
                    filterKetAktif = ket.toLowerCase();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
                    btn.classList.add('active');
                    terapkanFilterLokal();
                }
        
                function terapkanFilterLokal() {
                    const cariEl = document.getElementById('cariLokal');
                    const keyword = cariEl ? cariEl.value.toLowerCase() : '';
                    let urut = 0;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.baris-lokal').forEach(tr => {
                        const ket = tr.dataset.ket.toLowerCase();
                        const cocokKet = (filterKetAktif === 'all' || ket.includes(filterKetAktif));
                        const cocokKata = tr.dataset.search.includes(keyword);
        
                        if (cocokKet && cocokKata) {
                            tr.style.display = '';
                            urut++;
                            tr.querySelector('.nomor-lokal').innerText = urut;
                        } else {
                            tr.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    });
                }
        
                function isiNamaDariDropdown(selectEl, targetInputId) {
                    const opt = selectEl.options[selectEl.selectedIndex];
                    const nama = opt.getAttribute('data-nama');
                    if (nama) {
                        document.getElementById(targetInputId).value = nama;
                    }
                }
        
                function bukaModalTambahKelas() { document.getElementById('modalTambahKelas')?.classList.remove('hidden'); }
                function tutupModalTambahKelas() { document.getElementById('modalTambahKelas')?.classList.add('hidden'); }
        
                function bukaModalEditKelas(data) {
                    document.getElementById('edit_id_kelas').value = data.id;
                    document.getElementById('edit_nama_halaqah').value = data.nama_halaqah || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_id_ustadz').value = data.id_ustadz || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_nama_pengajar').value = data.nama_user_ustadz || data.nama_pengajar || '';
                    document.getElementById('edit_keterangan').value = data.keterangan || '';
                    document.getElementById('modalEditKelas')?.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
                function tutupModalEditKelas() { document.getElementById('modalEditKelas')?.classList.add('hidden'); }
        
                function bukaModalAssignSantri() { document.getElementById('modalAssignSantri')?.classList.remove('hidden'); }
                function tutupModalAssignSantri() { document.getElementById('modalAssignSantri')?.classList.add('hidden'); }
        
        window.filterKategoriLokal = filterKategoriLokal;
        window.terapkanFilterLokal = terapkanFilterLokal;
        window.isiNamaDariDropdown = isiNamaDariDropdown;
        window.bukaModalTambahKelas = bukaModalTambahKelas;
        window.tutupModalTambahKelas = tutupModalTambahKelas;
        window.bukaModalEditKelas = bukaModalEditKelas;
        window.tutupModalEditKelas = tutupModalEditKelas;
        window.bukaModalAssignSantri = bukaModalAssignSantri;
        window.tutupModalAssignSantri = tutupModalAssignSantri;
    },
    'dashboard': () => {
        const d = readPageData();
        const trend = document.getElementById('progresChart');
        if (window.Chart && trend) new Chart(trend, {
            type: 'line',
            data: { labels: d.chart_labels || [], datasets: [
                { label: 'Ziyadah (Ayat)', data: d.chart_ziyadah || [], borderColor: '#10b981', backgroundColor: 'rgba(16,185,129,.12)', borderWidth: 2.5, tension: .4, fill: true, pointRadius: 4, pointBackgroundColor: '#10b981' },
                { label: "Muraja'ah (Kali)", data: d.chart_murajaah || [], borderColor: '#3b82f6', backgroundColor: 'rgba(59,130,246,.08)', borderWidth: 2.5, tension: .4, fill: true, pointRadius: 4, pointBackgroundColor: '#3b82f6' }
            ] },

        function hapusPojok(btn) { btn.closest('.pojok-item').remove(); updateNomorPojok(); }
        function tambahPojok(namaDefault = '', nilaiDefault = '') {
            const container = document.getElementById('pojok-container');
            if (!container) return;
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'pojok-item p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl relative group transition hover:border-indigo-400 hover:bg-white';
            div.innerHTML = '<button type="button" onclick="hapusPojok(this)" class="absolute top-2 right-2 text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-500 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition"><i data-lucide="x-circle" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>' +
                '<label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 mb-1">Materi <span class="nomor-pojok"></span></label>' +
                '<input type="text" name="materi[]" value="' + namaDefault + '" required class="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 mb-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 outline-none" placeholder="Nama Surat / Halaman">' +
                '<input type="number" name="nilai[]" value="' + nilaiDefault + '" min="0" max="100" placeholder="Nilai (0-100)" class="input-nilai-rapor w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-center text-indigo-700 outline-none">';
            container.appendChild(div); updateNomorPojok(); if (window.lucide) window.lucide.createIcons();
        }
        function updateNomorPojok() { document.querySelectorAll('.nomor-pojok').forEach((el,i) => el.innerText=i+1); }
        function muatPreset37Surat() { if (!confirm('Reset tampilan form menjadi 37 Surat Juz 30 standar?')) return; const c=document.getElementById('pojok-container'); if(c){c.innerHTML='';surat.forEach(s=>tambahPojok(s,''));} }
        function muatPreset20Pojok(juz) { if (!confirm('Reset tampilan form menjadi 20 Pojok standar untuk Juz '+juz+'?')) return; const c=document.getElementById('pojok-container'); if(!c)return; c.innerHTML=''; for(let i=1;i<=20;i++){const hal=(juz-1)*20+i+1;tambahPojok((juz===1&&i===1)?'Al-fatihah + Halaman 2':'Halaman '+hal,'');} }
        function isiNilaiMassal(angka) { document.querySelectorAll('.input-nilai-rapor').forEach(inp=>{if(inp.value==='')inp.value=angka;}); }
        Object.assign(window,{hapusPojok,tambahPojok,updateNomorPojok,muatPreset37Surat,muatPreset20Pojok,isiNilaiMassal});
    },

    'login': () => {
        function togglePassword() {
                    const input = document.getElementById('inputPassword');
                    const eyeOpen = document.getElementById('eyeOpen');
                    const eyeClosed = document.getElementById('eyeClosed');
        
                    if (input.type === 'password') {
                        input.type = 'text';
                        eyeOpen.classList.add('hidden');
                        eyeClosed.classList.remove('hidden');
                    } else {
                        input.type = 'password';
                        eyeOpen.classList.remove('hidden');
                        eyeClosed.classList.add('hidden');
                    }
                }
        
        window.togglePassword = togglePassword;
    },

===== config/database.php =====
<?php
$host     = "localhost";
$username = "root";       
$password = "";  
$database = "hafalan_santri";

$conn = new mysqli($host, $username, $password, $database);

if ($conn->connect_error) {
    die("Koneksi database gagal: " . $conn->connect_error);
}

$conn->set_charset("utf8mb4");

?>

===== includes/auth.php =====
<?php
/** Shared session helpers for SIRAH pages and API handlers. */
function startAppSession(): void
{
    if (session_status() !== PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) {
        session_start();
    }
}

function isAuthenticated(): bool
{
    return isset($_SESSION['user_id']);
}

function userHasRole(array $roles): bool
{
    return isset($_SESSION['role']) && in_array($_SESSION['role'], $roles, true);
}

===== includes/functions.php =====
<?php
/** Shared application helpers for permissions, santri filters, and notifications. */
// 5. Fungsi Helper Cek Izin Akses File
function cekIzinAksesFile($conn, $role, $nama_file) {
    if ($role === 'operator') return true;
    if (in_array($nama_file, ['login.php', 'proses_login.php', 'logout.php', 'dashboard.php', 'index.php', 'profil_saya.php'])) return true;

    $kolom_role = [
        'pimpinan' => 'akses_pimpinan',
        'ustadz'   => 'akses_ustadz',
        'ortu'     => 'akses_ortu'
    ][$role] ?? '';

    if (empty($kolom_role)) return false;

    $file_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $nama_file);
    $q = "SELECT $kolom_role FROM hak_akses_menu 
          WHERE file_utama = '$file_esc' OR FIND_IN_SET('$file_esc', file_terkait) > 0";
    $res = mysqli_query($conn, $q);

    if ($res && mysqli_num_rows($res) > 0) {
        while ($r = mysqli_fetch_assoc($res)) {
            if ((int)$r[$kolom_role] === 1) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// 6. Fungsi Pintar: Deteksi apakah user yang login adalah Ustadzah (Perempuan) atau Ustadz (Laki-laki)
function isPengajarUstadzah($conn, $id_user) {
    $id_user = (int) $id_user;
    if ($id_user <= 0) return false;

    $q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT u.jenis_kelamin, u.nama_lengkap, u.username, h.nama_halaqah 
                              FROM users u 
                              LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON (u.id_halaqah = h.id OR h.id_ustadz = u.id) 
                              WHERE u.id = $id_user LIMIT 1");
    if ($q && $row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q)) {
        // Cek 1: Kolom jenis_kelamin di tabel users = 'P'
        if (($row['jenis_kelamin'] ?? '') === 'P') return true;
        // Cek 2: Username diawali 'ustadzah' atau nama mengandung 'ustadzah'
        if (stripos($row['username'] ?? '', 'ustadzah') !== false) return true;
        if (stripos($row['nama_lengkap'] ?? '', 'ustadzah') !== false) return true;
        // Cek 3: Mengajar di lokal yang namanya mengandung 'Perempuan' atau 'Akhwat'
        if (stripos($row['nama_halaqah'] ?? '', 'perempuan') !== false || stripos($row['nama_halaqah'] ?? '', 'akhwat') !== false) {
            // Sekalian perbaiki kolom jenis_kelamin di database agar permanen 'P'
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET jenis_kelamin = 'P' WHERE id = $id_user");
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

// 7. Fungsi Terpusat: Ambil SQL Filter Santri Khusus Binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah
function getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $id_user, $role, $alias_tabel_santri = 's') {
    // Operator & Pimpinan bisa melihat seluruh santri
    if (in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan'])) {
        return "";
    }

    // Mode Badal: Ustadz/Ustadzah menampilkan semua santri (?semua=1)
    if (isset($_GET['semua']) && $_GET['semua'] === '1') {
        return "";
    }

    $id_user = (int) $id_user;
    $u_info = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id_halaqah, nama_lengkap, jenis_kelamin FROM users WHERE id = $id_user"));
    $id_h_user = (int) ($u_info['id_halaqah'] ?? 0);
    $nm_esc    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($u_info['nama_lengkap'] ?? ''));

    // Cari semua ID lokal yang diampu oleh Ustadz/Ustadzah ini
    $q_lokal = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_halaqah FROM halaqah 
                                    WHERE id_ustadz = $id_user 
                                       OR id = $id_h_user 
                                       OR (nama_pengajar IS NOT NULL AND nama_pengajar != '' AND LOWER(nama_pengajar) = LOWER('$nm_esc'))");
    $lokal_ids = [];
    if ($q_lokal) {
        while ($lk = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_lokal)) {
            $lokal_ids[] = (int) $lk['id'];
        }
    }

    $prefix = !empty($alias_tabel_santri) ? "$alias_tabel_santri." : "";

    // Jika ustadz/ustadzah sudah punya lokal binaan, filter HANYA santri di lokal tersebut!
    if (!empty($lokal_ids)) {
        $list_id = implode(',', $lokal_ids);
        return " WHERE {$prefix}id_halaqah IN ($list_id) ";
    }

    // Jika ustadz/ustadzah belum punya lokal spesifik, filter berdasarkan santri yang id_ustadz = beliau ATAU sesuai jenis kelaminnya (Putri utk Ustadzah, Putra utk Ustadz)
    $is_ustadzah = isPengajarUstadzah($conn, $id_user);
    $jk_target   = $is_ustadzah ? 'P' : 'L';
    return " WHERE ({$prefix}id_ustadz = $id_user OR ({$prefix}id_halaqah IS NULL AND {$prefix}jenis_kelamin = '$jk_target')) ";
}

// 8. Fungsi Helper: Buat Link WhatsApp Otomatis untuk Laporan Setoran & PR ke Orang Tua
function buatLinkWaHafalan($no_hp, $nama_santri, $nama_ortu, $jenis, $tanggal, $juz, $pojok, $surat, $ayat_awal, $ayat_akhir, $predikat, $catatan, $nama_pengajar = 'Ustadz/Ustadzah') {
    $hp = preg_replace('/[^0-9]/', '', $no_hp ?? '');
    if (empty($hp)) return '';
    if (strpos($hp, '0') === 0) {
        $hp = '62' . substr($hp, 1);
    }

    $kat = strtoupper($jenis);
    $tgl_indo = date('d/m/Y', strtotime($tanggal));
    $ortu_sapaan = !empty($nama_ortu) ? "Bapak/Ibu *$nama_ortu*" : "Orang Tua/Wali";
    $info_juz = !empty($juz) ? "Juz $juz" . (!empty($pojok) ? " ($pojok)" : "") : "-";
    $info_ayat = ($jenis === 'tasmi') ? "Ujian Sekali Duduk" : "QS. $surat : Ayat $ayat_awal - $ayat_akhir";
    $ket_predikat = ($predikat === 'A') ? "A (Sangat Baik / Lancar)" : (($predikat === 'B') ? "B (Lancar dengan Catatan)" : "C (Perlu Mengulang)");
    $cat_teks = !empty($catatan) ? $catatan : "Alhamdulillah lancar, mohon terus dimotivasi di rumah.";

    $pesan  = "Assalamu'alaikum Warahmatullahi Wabarakatuh,\n";
    $pesan .= "Yth. $ortu_sapaan,\n\n";
    $pesan .= "Berikut laporan setoran *$kat* Ananda di *Surau Tahfizh Firdaus*:\n";
    $pesan .= "----------------------------------\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Nama Santri:* $nama_santri\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Tanggal:* $tgl_indo\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Juz / Pojok:* $info_juz\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Materi:* $info_ayat\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Predikat:* *$ket_predikat*\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Catatan:* _\"$cat_teks\"_\n";
    $pesan .= "----------------------------------\n";
    $pesan .= "Disimak oleh: *$nama_pengajar*\n";
    $pesan .= "Terima kasih atas kerja sama Ayah/Bunda dalam mendampingi muroja'ah Ananda di rumah.";

    return "https://wa.me/$hp?text=" . rawurlencode($pesan);
}

function buatLinkWaPR($no_hp, $nama_santri, $nama_ortu, $tanggal, $judul, $deskripsi, $nama_pengajar = 'Ustadz/Ustadzah') {
    $hp = preg_replace('/[^0-9]/', '', $no_hp ?? '');
    if (empty($hp)) return '';
    if (strpos($hp, '0') === 0) {
        $hp = '62' . substr($hp, 1);
    }

    $tgl_indo = date('d/m/Y', strtotime($tanggal));
    $ortu_sapaan = !empty($nama_ortu) ? "Bapak/Ibu *$nama_ortu*" : "Orang Tua/Wali";

    $pesan  = "Assalamu'alaikum Warahmatullahi Wabarakatuh,\n";
    $pesan .= "Yth. $ortu_sapaan,\n\n";
    $pesan .= "Terdapat *Tugas Rumah (PR)* untuk Ananda *$nama_santri* dari *Surau Tahfizh Firdaus*:\n";
    $pesan .= "----------------------------------\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Tanggal:* $tgl_indo\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Judul Tugas:* $judul\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Instruksi:* $deskripsi\n";
    $pesan .= "----------------------------------\n";
    $pesan .= "Mohon bantuan Ayah/Bunda untuk menyimak tugas Ananda di rumah dan menandainya selesai di Portal Orang Tua. Terima kasih.\n";
    $pesan .= "— *$nama_pengajar*";

    return "https://wa.me/$hp?text=" . rawurlencode($pesan);
}

// 9. Fungsi Render Tombol Switcher "Khusus Lokal Binaan" vs "Tampilkan Semua Santri (Badal)"
if (!function_exists('renderBadgeFilterBinaan')) {
    function renderBadgeFilterBinaan($role) {
        if (in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan'])) return '';

        $is_semua = (isset($_GET['semua']) && $_GET['semua'] === '1');
        $params   = $_GET;
        $params['semua'] = $is_semua ? '0' : '1';
        $url_toggle = basename($_SERVER['PHP_SELF']) . '?' . http_build_query($params);

        if ($is_semua) {
            return '
            <a href="' . htmlspecialchars($url_toggle) . '" title="Kembali ke santri lokal binaan saya"
               class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 hover:bg-amber-100 transition shadow-sm">
                <i data-lucide="users" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                <span>Mode Badal: Semua Santri</span>
                <span class="underline ml-1 font-bold">Reset ke Binaan</span>
            </a>';
        }
        return '
            <a href="' . htmlspecialchars($url_toggle) . '" title="Klik jika Anda sedang menggantikan ustadz/ah lain (Badal)"
               class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 hover:bg-emerald-100 transition shadow-sm">
                <i data-lucide="user-check" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                <span>Filter: Santri Binaan Saya</span>
                <span class="text-slate-400">|</span>
                <span class="underline text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900">Tampilkan Semua</span>
            </a>';
    }
}

===== includes/init_rbac.php =====
<?php
// Pastikan koneksi database sudah ada
if (!isset($conn)) {
    require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/database.php';
}

// 1. Pastikan ENUM role di tabel `users` mendukung 'operator'
$cek_role_col = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM users LIKE 'role'");
if ($cek_role_col) {
    $col_info = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_role_col);
    if (stripos($col_info['Type'], 'operator') === false) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE users MODIFY COLUMN `role` ENUM('operator','pimpinan','ustadz','ortu') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ustadz'");
    }
}

// Pastikan kolom jenis_kelamin & id_halaqah ada di tabel users
$cek_jk_u = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM users LIKE 'jenis_kelamin'");
if ($cek_jk_u && mysqli_num_rows($cek_jk_u) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN jenis_kelamin ENUM('L','P') DEFAULT 'L' AFTER nama_lengkap");
}

// 2. Buat akun Operator Default jika belum ada sama sekali (user: operator | pass: operator123)
$cek_op = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE role = 'operator' LIMIT 1");
if ($cek_op && mysqli_num_rows($cek_op) === 0) {
    $pass_op = password_hash('operator123', PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
    mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) VALUES ('operator', '$pass_op', 'Operator Utama Sistem', 'operator')");
}

// 3. Buat tabel `hak_akses_menu` untuk mengatur izin menu per aktor secara dinamis

  `akses_ortu` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `unik_kode_menu` (`kode_menu`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4");

$total_menu_db = $cek_isi_menu ? (int)mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_isi_menu)['total'] : 0;

        $icon  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $m[5]);
        $warna = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $m[6]);
        $urut  = (int)$m[7];
        $op    = (int)$m[8];
        $pimp  = (int)$m[9];
        $ust   = (int)$m[10];
        $ortu  = (int)$m[11];

        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT IGNORE INTO hak_akses_menu 
            (kode_menu, nama_menu, file_utama, file_terkait, kategori, icon, warna_icon, urutan, akses_operator, akses_pimpinan, akses_ustadz, akses_ortu)
            VALUES ('$kode', '$nama', '$utama', '$terk', '$kat', '$icon', '$warna', $urut, $op, $pimp, $ust, $ortu)");
    }
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/functions.php';
?>

===== includes/sidebar.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/init_rbac.php';

$role          = $_SESSION['role'] ?? '';
$nama          = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] ?? 'Pengguna';
$id_user_login = (int) ($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);
$halaman_aktif = basename($_SERVER['PHP_SELF']);

// Proteksi Otomatis: Cek apakah role yang sedang login diizinkan mengakses file halaman ini
if (!empty($role) && !cekIzinAksesFile($conn, $role, $halaman_aktif)) {
    $_SESSION['error'] = "Akses ditolak! Role Anda tidak memiliki izin untuk membuka halaman tersebut.";
    header("Location: dashboard.php");
    exit;
}

// Penyesuaian label role & warna badge (Deteksi Akurat Ustadz vs Ustadzah dari DB & Lokal)
$label_role = 'Pengguna';
$badge_role_class = 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border-slate-700';

if ($role === 'operator') {
    $label_role = 'Operator Sistem';
    $badge_role_class = 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/40';
} elseif ($role === 'ustadz') {
    if (isPengajarUstadzah($conn, $id_user_login)) {
        $label_role = 'Ustadzah';
        $badge_role_class = 'bg-teal-500/15 text-teal-300 border-teal-500/30';
    } else {
        $label_role = 'Ustadz';
        $badge_role_class = 'bg-indigo-500/15 text-indigo-300 border-indigo-500/30';
    }
} elseif ($role === 'ortu') {
    $label_role = 'Wali Santri';
    $badge_role_class = 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30';
} elseif ($role === 'pimpinan') {
    $label_role = 'Pimpinan';
    $badge_role_class = 'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30';
}

// Ambil daftar menu dari database `hak_akses_menu` sesuai Role yang sedang login
$kolom_akses = [
    'operator' => 'akses_operator',
    'pimpinan' => 'akses_pimpinan',
    'ustadz'   => 'akses_ustadz',
    'ortu'     => 'akses_ortu'
][$role] ?? 'akses_ortu';

$q_menu_sidebar = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM hak_akses_menu WHERE $kolom_akses = 1 ORDER BY urutan ASC");
$sidebar_menus = $q_menu_sidebar ? mysqli_fetch_all($q_menu_sidebar, MYSQLI_ASSOC) : [];

$grouped_sidebar = [];
foreach ($sidebar_menus as $sm) {
    $grouped_sidebar[$sm['kategori']][] = $sm;
}
?>
<aside class="w-64 bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 flex flex-col h-screen fixed left-0 top-0 border-r border-slate-800/80 shadow-2xl z-40 select-none">
    
    <!-- BRAND HEADER -->
    <div class="p-5 border-b border-slate-800/80 bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-900 to-slate-950">
        <a href="dashboard.php" class="flex items-center gap-3.5 group">
            <div class="relative w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500 to-indigo-700 p-0.5 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-950/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <div class="w-full h-full bg-slate-950 rounded-[10px] flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                    <i data-lucide="book-open-check" class="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-400"></i>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="min-w-0">
                <h1 class="text-sm font-extrabold text-white tracking-wide leading-tight group-hover:text-indigo-300 transition">
                    SURAU TAHFIZH
                </h1>
                <p class="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">Mushalla Firdaus</p>
            </div>
        </a>
    </div>

    <!-- DYNAMIC NAVIGATION LINKS BERDASARKAN HAK AKSES ACTOR -->
    <nav class="flex-1 py-5 px-3.5 overflow-y-auto sidebar-scroll space-y-5">
        <?php foreach ($grouped_sidebar as $nama_kategori => $items): ?>
        <div>
            <p class="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2 px-3">
                <?= htmlspecialchars($nama_kategori) ?>
            </p>
            <ul class="space-y-1">
                <?php foreach ($items as $item): 
                    $terkait_arr = array_filter(array_map('trim', explode(',', $item['file_terkait'] ?? '')));
                    $all_files   = array_merge([$item['file_utama']], $terkait_arr);
                    $aktif       = in_array($halaman_aktif, $all_files);
                    $cls_item    = $aktif 
                        ? 'relative bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600/90 to-indigo-600/70 text-white font-semibold shadow-md shadow-indigo-950/50 border border-indigo-400/20' 
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800/70 hover:text-slate-100 border border-transparent';
                ?>
                <li>
                    <a href="<?= htmlspecialchars($item['file_utama']) ?>" class="group flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all duration-150 <?= $cls_item ?>">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                            <?php if ($aktif): ?>
                                <span class="absolute left-0 top-2 bottom-2 w-1 bg-white rounded-r-full"></span>
                            <?php endif; ?>
                            <i data-lucide="<?= htmlspecialchars($item['icon']) ?>" class="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 <?= $aktif ? 'text-white' : htmlspecialchars($item['warna_icon']) ?> transition"></i>
                            <span class="truncate"><?= htmlspecialchars($item['nama_menu']) ?></span>
                        </div>
                        <?php if ($item['kode_menu'] === 'kelola_hak_akses'): ?>
                            <span class="text-[9px] font-extrabold uppercase px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-400/30">Admin</span>
                        <?php endif; ?>
                    </a>
                </li>
                <?php endforeach; ?>
            </ul>
        </div>
        <?php endforeach; ?>
    </nav>

    <!-- USER PROFILE & LOGOUT FOOTER -->
    <div class="p-3.5 border-t border-slate-800/80 bg-slate-900/60">
        <div class="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800/90 flex items-center gap-3 mb-2.5">
            <div class="relative shrink-0">
                <div class="w-9 h-9 bg-gradient-to-br <?= $role === 'operator' ? 'from-rose-500 to-orange-600' : ($label_role === 'Ustadzah' ? 'from-teal-500 to-emerald-600' : 'from-indigo-500 to-purple-600') ?> rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-sm">
                    <?= strtoupper(substr($nama, 0, 1)) ?>
                </div>
                <span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 bg-emerald-500 border-2 border-slate-900 rounded-full absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5"></span>

                <p class="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate" title="<?= htmlspecialchars($nama) ?>">
                    <?= htmlspecialchars($nama) ?>

                    <?= htmlspecialchars($label_role) ?>
                </span>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">

                <i data-lucide="user-cog" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>

<?php
/** Front controller for SIRAH pages. */
$routes = [
    'dashboard' => ['pages/dashboard/index.php', 'dashboard.php'],

    'absensi_anak' => ['pages/absensi/index.php', 'absensi_anak.php'],
