===== pages/absensi/index.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya untuk Role Orang Tua (ortu)
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'ortu') {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// Ambil daftar anak yang terhubung dengan akun orang tua ini
$anak_query = "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu ORDER BY nama ASC";
$anak_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $anak_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Pilih anak aktif (default: anak pertama)
$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($anak_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$santri = null;
foreach ($anak_list as $a) {
    if ($a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih) {
        $santri = $a;
        break;
    }
}

// Filter Bulan & Tahun (Default: Bulan & Tahun Saat Ini)
$bulan_pilih = isset($_GET['bulan']) ? str_pad((int)$_GET['bulan'], 2, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT) : date('m');
$tahun_pilih = isset($_GET['tahun']) ? (int) $_GET['tahun'] : (int) date('Y');
$periode     = "$tahun_pilih-$bulan_pilih";

$nama_bulan = [
    '01' => 'Januari', '02' => 'Februari', '03' => 'Maret', '04' => 'April',
    '05' => 'Mei', '06' => 'Juni', '07' => 'Juli', '08' => 'Agustus',
    '09' => 'September', '10' => 'Oktober', '11' => 'November', '12' => 'Desember'
];

$Nama_Hari_Indo = [
    'Sunday' => 'Minggu', 'Monday' => 'Senin', 'Tuesday' => 'Selasa',
    'Wednesday' => 'Rabu', 'Thursday' => 'Kamis', 'Friday' => 'Jumat', 'Saturday' => 'Sabtu'
];

$rekap = ['H' => 0, 'S' => 0, 'I' => 0, 'A' => 0];
$absensi_list = [];
$map_tanggal = []; // Untuk kalender visual

if ($santri) {
    $id_santri = (int) $santri['id'];

    // Ambil data absensi dari tabel `absensi` pada periode terpilih
    $q_absensi = "SELECT a.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz 
                  FROM absensi a 
                  LEFT JOIN users u ON a.id_ustadz = u.id 
                  WHERE a.id_santri = $id_santri 
                    AND DATE_FORMAT(a.tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$periode'
                  ORDER BY a.tanggal DESC, a.id DESC";
    $res_absensi = mysqli_query($conn, $q_absensi);
    if ($res_absensi) {
        $absensi_list = mysqli_fetch_all($res_absensi, MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }

    foreach ($absensi_list as $row) {
        $inisial = strtoupper(substr(trim($row['status']), 0, 1));
        if (isset($rekap[$inisial])) {
            $rekap[$inisial]++;
        }
        $tgl_angka = (int) date('j', strtotime($row['tanggal']));
        $map_tanggal[$tgl_angka] = [
            'inisial'    => $inisial,
            'keterangan' => $row['keterangan'] ?? '',
            'ustadz'     => $row['nama_ustadz'] ?? 'Ustadz/ah'
        ];
    }
}

$total_pertemuan = array_sum($rekap);
$persentase_hadir = $total_pertemuan > 0 ? round(($rekap['H'] / $total_pertemuan) * 100) : 0;

// Konfigurasi Kalender Bulan Terpilih
$jumlah_hari_bulan = cal_days_in_month(CAL_GREGORIAN, (int)$bulan_pilih, $tahun_pilih);
$hari_pertama_minggu = (int) date('N', strtotime("$tahun_pilih-$bulan_pilih-01")); // 1 (Senin) s/d 7 (Minggu)
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Kehadiran Anak - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <!-- Header & Filter Periode -->
        <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-end gap-4 mb-8">
            <div>
                <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div class="p-2.5 bg-blue-100 rounded-xl">
                        <i data-lucide="calendar-check" class="w-7 h-7 text-blue-600"></i>
                    </div>
                    Kehadiran & Presensi Ananda
                </h1>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">Pantau rekapitulasi kehadiran harian dan kedisiplinan ananda di Surau Tahfizh Firdaus.</p>
            </div>

            <!-- Filter Anak, Bulan & Tahun -->
            <form method="GET" action="absensi_anak.php" class="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-2.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <?php if (count($anak_list) > 1): ?>
                    <select name="id_santri" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                        <?php foreach ($anak_list as $a): ?>
                            <option value="<?= $a['id'] ?>" <?= $a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih ? 'selected' : '' ?>>
                                👦 <?= htmlspecialchars($a['nama']) ?>
                            </option>
                        <?php endforeach; ?>
                    </select>
                <?php endif; ?>

                <select name="bulan" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                    <?php foreach ($nama_bulan as $num => $nama_bln): ?>
                        <option value="<?= $num ?>" <?= $num == $bulan_pilih ? 'selected' : '' ?>><?= $nama_bln ?></option>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                </select>

                <select name="tahun" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                    <?php for ($th = date('Y') - 2; $th <= date('Y') + 1; $th++): ?>
                        <option value="<?= $th ?>" <?= $th == $tahun_pilih ? 'selected' : '' ?>><?= $th ?></option>
                    <?php endfor; ?>
                </select>
            </form>
        </div>

        <?php if (!$santri): ?>
            <div class="bg-amber-50 border-l-4 border-amber-500 text-amber-800 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm">
                <h3 class="font-bold text-base mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i data-lucide="alert-triangle" class="w-5 h-5"></i> Data Anak Belum Terhubung
                </h3>
                <p class="text-xs">Akun orang tua Anda belum ditautkan dengan data santri. Silakan hubungi Ustadz/Ustadzah pembimbing.</p>
            </div>
        <?php else: ?>

        <!-- KARTU IDENTITAS & PERSENTASE KEHADIRAN -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-8">
            
            <!-- Profil Ananda -->
            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4">
                <div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 text-white font-extrabold text-2xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-md shadow-blue-100">
                    <?= strtoupper(substr($santri['nama'], 0, 1)) ?>
                </div>
                <div class="min-w-0">
                    <span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-blue-600 bg-blue-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-blue-200">
                        Periode <?= $nama_bulan[$bulan_pilih] ?> <?= $tahun_pilih ?>
                    </span>
                    <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 truncate mt-1.5"><?= htmlspecialchars($santri['nama']) ?></h2>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">NIS: <?= htmlspecialchars($santri['nis'] ?: '-') ?> • <?= $santri['jenis_kelamin'] === 'L' ? 'Santriwan (Putra)' : 'Santriwati (Putri)' ?></p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Bar Persentase Kehadiran -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 mb-3">
                    <div>
                        <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Tingkat Kehadiran Bulan Ini</p>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                            <h3 class="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900"><?= $persentase_hadir ?>%</h3>
                            <?php if ($total_pertemuan === 0): ?>
                                <span class="text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-500">Belum ada presensi</span>
                            <?php elseif ($persentase_hadir >= 85): ?>
                                <span class="text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700">✨ Sangat Disiplin</span>
                            <?php elseif ($persentase_hadir >= 70): ?>
                                <span class="text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-100 text-amber-700">👍 Cukup Baik</span>
                            <?php else: ?>
                                <span class="text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-100 text-rose-700">⚠️ Perlu Perhatian</span>
                            <?php endif; ?>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-right">
                        <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">Total Hari Tercatat:</span>
                        <p class="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-800"><?= $total_pertemuan ?> Pertemuan</p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="w-full h-3.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 mb-2">
                    <div class="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-teal-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-500" style="width: <?= $persentase_hadir ?>%"></div>
                </div>

                <div class="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-500">
                    <span>Hadir: <b class="text-emerald-600"><?= $rekap['H'] ?> Hari</b></span>
                    <span>Tidak Hadir (S/I/A): <b class="text-slate-700"><?= $rekap['S'] + $rekap['I'] + $rekap['A'] ?> Hari</b></span>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 4 KARTU RINGKASAN STATUS -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5 mb-8">
            <div class="bg-white border border-emerald-200 border-b-4 border-b-emerald-500 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Hadir (H)</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $rekap['H'] ?></p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Mengikuti halaqah</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="user-check" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white border border-blue-200 border-b-4 border-b-blue-500 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-blue-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Sakit (S)</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $rekap['S'] ?></p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Izin karena sakit</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center">

                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $rekap['A'] ?></p>

                                    <td class="py-4 px-6">
                                        <p class="font-bold text-slate-800"><?= $hari_indo ?>, <?= date('d M Y', strtotime($a['tanggal'])) ?></p>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="py-4 px-4 text-center">
                                        <span class="inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold border <?= $badge ?>">
                                            <?= $label ?>
                                        </span>
                                    </td>

                                        <?= htmlspecialchars($a['nama_ustadz'] ?? 'Ustadz Pembimbing') ?>

                </div>
            </div>

    </div>

===== pages/dashboard/index.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$nama = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'];
$role = $_SESSION['role'];

$chart_labels = [];
$chart_ziyadah = [];
$chart_murajaah = [];
$stat = ['total_ayat' => 0, 'total_murajaah' => 0, 'total_tasmi' => 0, 'santri_aktif' => 0];
$total_santri = 0;
$total_ustadz = 0;
$predikat_data = ['A' => 0, 'B' => 0, 'C' => 0];
$aktivitas_list = [];
$target_widget = ['total' => 0, 'tercapai' => 0, 'persentase' => 0, 'label' => ''];

if (in_array($role, ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    $total_santri = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM santri"))['total'];
    $total_ustadz = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM users WHERE role = 'ustadz'"))['total'];

    $bulan_ini = date('Y-m');
    $stat_query = "SELECT 
                    COUNT(DISTINCT id_santri) as santri_aktif,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ayat,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi
                  FROM hafalan 
                  WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'";
    $stat = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $stat_query));

    for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
        $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $q = "SELECT 
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as z,
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as m
              FROM hafalan WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'";
        $d = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $q));
        $chart_ziyadah[] = (int) $d['z'];
        $chart_murajaah[] = (int) $d['m'];
    }

    $predikat_res = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT predikat, COUNT(*) as jumlah FROM hafalan WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini' GROUP BY predikat");
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($predikat_res)) {
        if (isset($predikat_data[$row['predikat']])) {
            $predikat_data[$row['predikat']] = (int) $row['jumlah'];
        }
    }

    $target_widget['label'] = in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan']) ? 'Santri Mencapai Target Bulanan' : 'Realisasi Target Santri Binaan';
    $target_widget['total'] = $total_santri;
    $capaian_query = "SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT s.id) as tercapai
                      FROM santri s
                      JOIN target_hafalan t ON s.id = t.id_santri
                      LEFT JOIN (
                          SELECT id_santri, SUM(jumlah_ayat) as total_ayat
                          FROM hafalan 
                          WHERE jenis = 'ziyadah' AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'
                          GROUP BY id_santri
                      ) h ON s.id = h.id_santri
                      WHERE (t.target_ayat_per_hari * 20) <= COALESCE(h.total_ayat, 0)";
    $target_widget['tercapai'] = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $capaian_query))['tercapai'];
    $target_widget['persentase'] = $target_widget['total'] > 0 ? round(($target_widget['tercapai'] / $target_widget['total']) * 100) : 0;

    $aktivitas_query = "SELECT h.*, s.nama as nama_santri, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz
                        FROM hafalan h
                        JOIN santri s ON h.id_santri = s.id
                        LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
                        ORDER BY h.created_at DESC LIMIT 6";
    $aktivitas_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $aktivitas_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

} elseif ($role === 'ortu') {
    $id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
    $santri = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu LIMIT 1"));

    if ($santri) {
        $id_santri = $santri['id'];
        $bulan_ini = date('Y-m');

        $stat_query = "SELECT 
                        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ayat,
                        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
                        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi
                      FROM hafalan 
                      WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'";
        $stat = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $stat_query));

        for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
            $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
            $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
            $q = "SELECT 
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as z,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as m
                  FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'";
            $d = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $q));
            $chart_ziyadah[] = (int) $d['z'];
            $chart_murajaah[] = (int) $d['m'];
        }

        $target_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT target_ayat_per_hari FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1");
        $target_anak = ($target_q && mysqli_num_rows($target_q) > 0) ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($target_q) : null;
        $target_ayat_harian = $target_anak ? (int) $target_anak['target_ayat_per_hari'] : 0;
        
        $target_widget['label'] = 'Capaian Target Bulanan Ananda';
        $target_widget['total'] = $target_ayat_harian * 20;
        $target_widget['tercapai'] = (int) $stat['total_ayat'];
        $target_widget['persentase'] = $target_widget['total'] > 0 ? min(100, round(($target_widget['tercapai'] / $target_widget['total']) * 100)) : 0;

        $aktivitas_query = "SELECT h.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz
                            FROM hafalan h
                            LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
                            WHERE h.id_santri = $id_santri
                            ORDER BY h.created_at DESC LIMIT 6";
        $aktivitas_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $aktivitas_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }
}

===== pages/hafalan/input_pr.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

// Keamanan: Hanya Operator, Ustadz/ah, atau Pimpinan
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) { 
    header("Location: login.php"); 
    exit; 
}

$id_ustadz = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role      = $_SESSION['role'];
$is_pengelola_penuh = in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan']);

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom status_ortu di tabel pr jika belum ada
$cek_kolom_pr = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM pr LIKE 'status_ortu'");
if ($cek_kolom_pr && mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom_pr) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE pr ADD COLUMN status_ortu ENUM('Belum','Selesai') DEFAULT 'Belum' AFTER deskripsi");
}

// Hapus PR jika diminta
if (isset($_GET['hapus'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_GET['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM pr WHERE id = $id_hapus");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data Tugas Rumah (PR) berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: input_pr.php");
    exit;
}

// Filter santri khusus binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah (Operator & Pimpinan melihat semua)
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $id_ustadz, $role, 's');
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_binaan 
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Proses Simpan PR Baru
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['judul'])) {   
    $tanggal   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal'] ?? date('Y-m-d'));
    $judul     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['judul']));
    $deskripsi = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['deskripsi']));
    
    if ($_POST['id_santri'] === 'semua_binaan') {
        $jml_kirim = 0;
        foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
            $id_s = (int) $s['id'];
            mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO pr (id_santri, tanggal, judul, deskripsi, id_ustadz) VALUES ($id_s, '$tanggal', '$judul', '$deskripsi', $id_ustadz)");
            $jml_kirim++;
        }
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Tugas Rumah (PR) berhasil dikirimkan secara massal kepada $jml_kirim santri binaan!";
    } else {
        $id_santri = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO pr (id_santri, tanggal, judul, deskripsi, id_ustadz) VALUES ($id_santri, '$tanggal', '$judul', '$deskripsi', $id_ustadz)");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Tugas Rumah (PR) berhasil ditambahkan untuk santri terpilih!";
    }
    
    header("Location: input_pr.php"); 
    exit;
}

// Ambil riwayat PR yang diberikan (khusus santri binaan)
$and_binaan = !empty($where_binaan) ? str_replace('WHERE', 'AND', $where_binaan) : '';
$riwayat_query = "SELECT p.*, s.nama as nama_santri, s.nama_ortu, s.no_hp_ortu, hl.nama_halaqah 
                  FROM pr p 
                  JOIN santri s ON p.id_santri = s.id 
                  LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
                  WHERE 1=1 $and_binaan 
                  ORDER BY p.tanggal DESC, p.id DESC LIMIT 30";
$riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Hitung ringkasan status PR
$total_pr_tampil = count($riwayat_list);
$pr_sudah_disimak = 0;
foreach ($riwayat_list as $r) {
    if (($r['status_ortu'] ?? 'Belum') === 'Selesai') $pr_sudah_disimak++;
}

===== pages/hafalan/kehadiran.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// Keamanan: Hanya Operator, Ustadz/ah, dan Pimpinan
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ustadz = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role      = $_SESSION['role'];
$nama_user = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] ?? '';
$is_pengelola_penuh = in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan']);

// Pastikan kolom status di tabel absensi mendukung format lengkap ('Hadir','Sakit','Izin','Alfa')
// sekaligus konversi data lama ('H','S','I','A') jika pernah tersimpan
mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE absensi MODIFY COLUMN `status` VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Hadir'");
mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = 'Hadir' WHERE status = 'H' OR status = ''");
mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = 'Sakit' WHERE status = 'S'");
mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = 'Izin'  WHERE status = 'I'");
mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = 'Alfa'  WHERE status = 'A'");

// Proses simpan absensi
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['status'])) {
    $tanggal  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal']);
    $id_lokal = isset($_POST['id_lokal']) ? (int) $_POST['id_lokal'] : 0;

    $map_lengkap = [
        'H' => 'Hadir', 'Hadir' => 'Hadir',
        'S' => 'Sakit', 'Sakit' => 'Sakit',
        'I' => 'Izin',  'Izin'  => 'Izin',
        'A' => 'Alfa',  'Alfa'  => 'Alfa'
    ];

    foreach ($_POST['status'] as $id_santri => $st_raw) {
        $id_s       = (int) $id_santri;
        $status_fix = $map_lengkap[$st_raw] ?? 'Hadir';
        $keterangan = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['keterangan'][$id_s] ?? ''));
        
        $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM absensi WHERE id_santri = $id_s AND tanggal = '$tanggal'");
        if ($cek && mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = '$status_fix', keterangan = '$keterangan', id_ustadz = $id_ustadz WHERE id_santri = $id_s AND tanggal = '$tanggal'");
        } else {
            mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO absensi (id_santri, tanggal, status, keterangan, id_ustadz) VALUES ($id_s, '$tanggal', '$status_fix', '$keterangan', $id_ustadz)");
        }
    }
    
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data kehadiran tanggal " . date('d F Y', strtotime($tanggal)) . " berhasil disimpan!";
    $q_lokal = $id_lokal > 0 ? "&id_lokal=$id_lokal" : "";
    header("Location: input_kehadiran.php?tanggal=$tanggal" . $q_lokal);
    exit;
}

// Ambil tanggal (default hari ini)
$tanggal  = $_GET['tanggal'] ?? date('Y-m-d');
$id_lokal = isset($_GET['id_lokal']) ? (int) $_GET['id_lokal'] : 0;

// Ambil daftar lokal (Jika Ustadz, utamakan lokal binaannya)
$where_lokal_opsi = "";
if (!$is_pengelola_penuh) {
    $u_info = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id_halaqah, nama_lengkap FROM users WHERE id = $id_ustadz"));
    $id_h_user = (int) ($u_info['id_halaqah'] ?? 0);
    $nm_esc    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($u_info['nama_lengkap'] ?? $nama_user));
    $where_lokal_opsi = "WHERE (id_ustadz = $id_ustadz OR id = $id_h_user OR LOWER(nama_pengajar) = LOWER('$nm_esc'))";
}
$lokal_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_halaqah FROM halaqah $where_lokal_opsi ORDER BY id ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Jika Ustadz hanya mengampu 1 lokal dan belum memilih filter, otomatis pilih lokalnya
if ($id_lokal === 0 && !$is_pengelola_penuh && count($lokal_list) === 1) {
    $id_lokal = (int) $lokal_list[0]['id'];
}

// Ambil daftar santri sesuai filter lokal
$where_santri = $id_lokal > 0 ? "WHERE s.id_halaqah = $id_lokal" : "";
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_santri 
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Ambil data absensi yang sudah diinput untuk tanggal tersebut
$absensi_hari_ini = [];
$statistik = ['H' => 0, 'S' => 0, 'I' => 0, 'A' => 0];

if (!empty($santri_list)) {
    $tgl_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $tanggal);
    $absensi_result = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM absensi WHERE tanggal = '$tgl_esc'");
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($absensi_result)) {
        $absensi_hari_ini[$row['id_santri']] = $row;
    }

    foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
        if (isset($absensi_hari_ini[$s['id']])) {
            $in = strtoupper(substr(trim($absensi_hari_ini[$s['id']]['status']), 0, 1));
            if (isset($statistik[$in])) $statistik[$in]++;
        }
    }
}
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Input Kehadiran - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

<?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

<div class="ml-64 p-8">
    
    <!-- Header Halaman -->
    <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-end gap-4 mb-8">
        <div>
            <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-3">
                <div class="p-2.5 bg-indigo-100 rounded-xl"><i data-lucide="calendar-check" class="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600"></i></div>

                        $raw_st = $absensi_hari_ini[$s['id']]['status'] ?? 'Hadir';

                                <?php if (!empty($s['nama_halaqah'])): ?>
                                    <span class="text-[10px] font-semibold bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded"><?= htmlspecialchars($s['nama_halaqah']) ?></span>
                                <?php endif; ?>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4">
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 w-max mx-auto">
                                <?php foreach ($opsi_status as $st): ?>
                                <label class="status-radio cursor-pointer">
                                    <input type="radio" name="status[<?= $s['id'] ?>]" value="<?= $st ?>" <?= $status_aktif === $st ? 'checked' : '' ?> required>
                                    <div class="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 transition-colors border border-transparent">
                                        <?= strtoupper($st) ?>
                                    </div>
                                </label>
                                <?php endforeach; ?>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-6">
                            <input type="text" name="keterangan[<?= $s['id'] ?>]" 
                                value="<?= htmlspecialchars($keterangan_aktif) ?>" 
                                class="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none transition" 
                                placeholder="Cth: Demam / Izin acara keluarga...">
                        </td>
                    </tr>

===== pages/hafalan/murajaah.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Pastikan kolom juz & pojok ada di tabel hafalan
$cek_juz = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM hafalan LIKE 'juz'");
if ($cek_juz && mysqli_num_rows($cek_juz) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `juz` INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `jenis`");
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `pojok` VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `juz`");
}

// Hapus riwayat setoran murajaah jika diminta
if (isset($_GET['hapus'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_GET['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM hafalan WHERE id = $id_hapus AND jenis = 'murajaah'");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data setoran muraja'ah berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: input_murajaah.php");
    exit;
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $_SESSION['user_id'], $_SESSION['role'], 's');

// Ambil daftar santri khusus lokal binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah yang login
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_binaan
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_result = mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($santri_result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Ambil riwayat murajaah hari ini (khusus santri binaan)
$jenis_halaman = 'murajaah'; 
$and_binaan = !empty($where_binaan) ? str_replace('WHERE', 'AND', $where_binaan) : '';
$riwayat_query = "SELECT h.*, s.nama as nama_santri, s.nama_ortu, s.no_hp_ortu, hl.nama_halaqah 
                  FROM hafalan h 
                  JOIN santri s ON h.id_santri = s.id 
                  LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
                  WHERE h.tanggal = CURDATE() AND h.jenis = '$jenis_halaman' $and_binaan
                  ORDER BY h.id DESC";
$riwayat_result = mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query);
$riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all($riwayat_result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Hitung ringkasan hari ini
$total_setoran_hari_ini = count($riwayat_list);
$total_ayat_hari_ini = 0;
foreach ($riwayat_list as $r) {
    $total_ayat_hari_ini += (int)($r['jumlah_ayat'] ?? 0);
}

// Daftar 114 Surat untuk Autocomplete
$daftar_surat = ["Al-Fatihah","Al-Baqarah","Ali 'Imran","An-Nisa'","Al-Ma'idah","Al-An'am","Al-A'raf","Al-Anfal","At-Taubah","Yunus","Hud","Yusuf","Ar-Ra'd","Ibrahim","Al-Hijr","An-Nahl","Al-Isra'","Al-Kahf","Maryam","Taha","Al-Anbiya'","Al-Hajj","Al-Mu'minun","An-Nur","Al-Furqan","Asy-Syu'ara'","An-Naml","Al-Qasas","Al-'Ankabut","Ar-Rum","Luqman","As-Sajdah","Al-Ahzab","Saba'","Fatir","Yasin","As-Saffat","Sad","Az-Zumar","Gafir","Fussilat","Asy-Syura","Az-Zukhruf","Ad-Dukhan","Al-Jasiyah","Al-Ahqaf","Muhammad","Al-Fath","Al-Hujurat","Qaf","Az-Zariyat","At-Tur","An-Najm","Al-Qamar","Ar-Rahman","Al-Waqi'ah","Al-Hadid","Al-Mujadilah","Al-Hasyr","Al-Mumtahanah","As-Saff","Al-Jumu'ah","Al-Munafiqun","At-Tagabun","At-Talaq","At-Tahrim","Al-Mulk","Al-Qalam","Al-Haqqah","Al-Ma'arij","Nuh","Al-Jinn","Al-Muzzammil","Al-Muddassir","Al-Qiyamah","Al-Insan","Al-Mursalat","An-Naba'","An-Nazi'at","'Abasa","At-Takwir","Al-Infitar","Al-Mutaffifin","Al-Insyiqaq","Al-Buruj","At-Tariq","Al-A'la","Al-Gasyiyah","Al-Fajr","Al-Balad","Asy-Syams","Al-Lail","Ad-Duha","Asy-Syarh","At-Tin","Al-'Alaq","Al-Qadr","Al-Bayyinah","Az-Zalzalah","Al-'Adiyat","Al-Qari'ah","At-Takasur","Al-'Asr","Al-Humazah","Al-Fil","Quraisy","Al-Ma'un","Al-Kausar","Al-Kafirun","An-Nasr","Al-Lahab","Al-Ikhlas","Al-Falaq","An-Nas"];
?>

$page_title = "Input Muraja'ah - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";

===== pages/hafalan/tasmi.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ustadz = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role      = $_SESSION['role'];

// Pastikan kolom juz & pojok ada di tabel hafalan
$cek_juz = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM hafalan LIKE 'juz'");
if ($cek_juz && mysqli_num_rows($cek_juz) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `juz` INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `jenis`");
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `pojok` VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `juz`");
}

// Hapus riwayat tasmi jika diminta
if (isset($_GET['hapus'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_GET['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM hafalan WHERE id = $id_hapus AND jenis = 'tasmi'");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data ujian tasmi' berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: input_tasmi.php");
    exit;
}

// Filter santri khusus lokal binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $id_ustadz, $role, 's');
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_binaan 
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Riwayat ujian tasmi (menampilkan 25 ujian terakhir dari santri binaan)
$and_binaan = !empty($where_binaan) ? str_replace('WHERE', 'AND', $where_binaan) : '';
$riwayat_query = "SELECT h.*, s.nama as nama_santri, s.nama_ortu, s.no_hp_ortu, hl.nama_halaqah 
                  FROM hafalan h 
                  JOIN santri s ON h.id_santri = s.id 
                  LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
                  WHERE h.jenis = 'tasmi' $and_binaan 
                  ORDER BY h.tanggal DESC, h.id DESC LIMIT 25";
$riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Input Tasmi' - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

                <div class="w-11 h-11 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center text-purple-600">
                    <i data-lucide="award" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Input Ujian Tasmi'</h1>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Pencatatan hasil ujian hafalan sekali duduk (tasmi') santri per Juz dan Pojok.</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                <?= renderBadgeFilterBinaan($_SESSION['role']) ?>
                <button type="button" onclick="tampilkanNotifPanduan()" class="bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition">
                    <i data-lucide="help-circle" class="w-4 h-4 text-purple-600"></i>
                    <span>Panduan Nilai</span>

===== pages/hafalan/ziyadah.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Pastikan kolom juz & pojok ada agar query tabel riwayat selalu aman
$cek_juz = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM hafalan LIKE 'juz'");
if ($cek_juz && mysqli_num_rows($cek_juz) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `juz` INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `jenis`");
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `pojok` VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `juz`");
}

// Hapus riwayat setoran jika diminta
if (isset($_GET['hapus'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_GET['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM hafalan WHERE id = $id_hapus AND jenis = 'ziyadah'");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data setoran ziyadah berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: input_ziyadah.php");
    exit;
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $_SESSION['user_id'], $_SESSION['role'], 's');

// Ambil daftar santri khusus lokal binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah yang login
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_binaan
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_result = mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($santri_result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Ambil riwayat ziyadah hari ini (khusus santri binaan)
$jenis_halaman = 'ziyadah'; 
$and_binaan = !empty($where_binaan) ? str_replace('WHERE', 'AND', $where_binaan) : '';
$riwayat_query = "SELECT h.*, s.nama as nama_santri, s.nama_ortu, s.no_hp_ortu, hl.nama_halaqah 
                  FROM hafalan h 
                  JOIN santri s ON h.id_santri = s.id 
                  LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
                  WHERE h.tanggal = CURDATE() AND h.jenis = '$jenis_halaman' $and_binaan
                  ORDER BY h.id DESC";
$riwayat_result = mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query);
$riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all($riwayat_result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Hitung ringkasan hari ini
$total_setoran_hari_ini = count($riwayat_list);
$total_ayat_hari_ini = 0;
foreach ($riwayat_list as $r) {
    $total_ayat_hari_ini += (int)($r['jumlah_ayat'] ?? 0);
}

// Daftar 114 Surat untuk Autocomplete
$daftar_surat = ["Al-Fatihah","Al-Baqarah","Ali 'Imran","An-Nisa'","Al-Ma'idah","Al-An'am","Al-A'raf","Al-Anfal","At-Taubah","Yunus","Hud","Yusuf","Ar-Ra'd","Ibrahim","Al-Hijr","An-Nahl","Al-Isra'","Al-Kahf","Maryam","Taha","Al-Anbiya'","Al-Hajj","Al-Mu'minun","An-Nur","Al-Furqan","Asy-Syu'ara'","An-Naml","Al-Qasas","Al-'Ankabut","Ar-Rum","Luqman","As-Sajdah","Al-Ahzab","Saba'","Fatir","Yasin","As-Saffat","Sad","Az-Zumar","Gafir","Fussilat","Asy-Syura","Az-Zukhruf","Ad-Dukhan","Al-Jasiyah","Al-Ahqaf","Muhammad","Al-Fath","Al-Hujurat","Qaf","Az-Zariyat","At-Tur","An-Najm","Al-Qamar","Ar-Rahman","Al-Waqi'ah","Al-Hadid","Al-Mujadilah","Al-Hasyr","Al-Mumtahanah","As-Saff","Al-Jumu'ah","Al-Munafiqun","At-Tagabun","At-Talaq","At-Tahrim","Al-Mulk","Al-Qalam","Al-Haqqah","Al-Ma'arij","Nuh","Al-Jinn","Al-Muzzammil","Al-Muddassir","Al-Qiyamah","Al-Insan","Al-Mursalat","An-Naba'","An-Nazi'at","'Abasa","At-Takwir","Al-Infitar","Al-Mutaffifin","Al-Insyiqaq","Al-Buruj","At-Tariq","Al-A'la","Al-Gasyiyah","Al-Fajr","Al-Balad","Asy-Syams","Al-Lail","Ad-Duha","Asy-Syarh","At-Tin","Al-'Alaq","Al-Qadr","Al-Bayyinah","Az-Zalzalah","Al-'Adiyat","Al-Qari'ah","At-Takasur","Al-'Asr","Al-Humazah","Al-Fil","Quraisy","Al-Ma'un","Al-Kausar","Al-Kafirun","An-Nasr","Al-Lahab","Al-Ikhlas","Al-Falaq","An-Nas"];
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Input Ziyadah - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';

        <?php endif; ?>

        <?php if (empty($santri_list)): ?>
            <div class="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-800 p-4 rounded-xl mb-6 text-xs font-medium flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <i data-lucide="alert-triangle" class="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600"></i>

===== pages/halaqah/index.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// Keamanan: Hanya Operator, Pimpinan, dan Ustadz/Ustadzah yang dapat mengakses
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'pimpinan', 'ustadz'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$role      = $_SESSION['role'];
$id_user   = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$nama_user = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] ?? '';

// Apakah pengguna adalah Pengelola Penuh (Operator / Pimpinan)?
$is_pengelola_penuh = in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan']);

// Pastikan kolom `keterangan` dan `nama_pengajar` tersedia di tabel `halaqah`
$cek_ket = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM halaqah LIKE 'keterangan'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_ket) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE halaqah ADD COLUMN keterangan VARCHAR(150) DEFAULT 'Tahfidz Sore ( 16.30 - Isya )' AFTER tingkat");
}
$cek_pengajar = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM halaqah LIKE 'nama_pengajar'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_pengajar) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE halaqah ADD COLUMN nama_pengajar VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL AFTER id_ustadz");
}

// === 1. FITUR 1-KLIK: MUAT 31 LOKAL DEFAULT (KHUSUS OPERATOR / PIMPINAN) ===

        ['Lokal Reguler 3 Perempuan', 'Junesa Yulanda', 'Tahfidz Sore ( 16.30 - Isya )'],
        ['Lokal C1', 'Vania Gusvarina, A.Md', 'Tahfidz Sore ( 16.30 - Sebelum Maghrib )'],
        ['Lokal C2', 'Thalia Adara Shakila', 'Tahfidz Sore ( 16.30 - Sebelum Maghrib )'],
        ['Lokal C3', 'Niswatun Fil Madina, S.Pd', 'Tahfidz Sore ( 16.30 - Sebelum Maghrib )']
    ];

    $berhasil = 0;
    foreach ($preset_lokal as $item) {
        $nm_lokal = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $item[0]);
        $nm_ust   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $item[1]);
        $ket_prog = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $item[2]);

        $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM halaqah WHERE nama_halaqah = '$nm_lokal'");
        if (mysqli_num_rows($cek) === 0) {
            mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO halaqah (nama_halaqah, nama_pengajar, keterangan) VALUES ('$nm_lokal', '$nm_ust', '$ket_prog')");
            $berhasil++;
        }
    }
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Berhasil memuat $berhasil daftar Kelas / Lokal standar beserta Ustadz/Ustadzah pengampunya.";
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php");
    exit;
}

// === 2. TAMBAH KELAS / LOKAL BARU (KHUSUS OPERATOR / PIMPINAN) ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['tambah_kelas']) && $is_pengelola_penuh) {
    $nama_halaqah  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_halaqah']));
    $id_ustadz     = !empty($_POST['id_ustadz']) ? (int) $_POST['id_ustadz'] : 'NULL';
    $nama_pengajar = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_pengajar'] ?? ''));
    $keterangan    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['keterangan']));

    if ($id_ustadz !== 'NULL' && empty($nama_pengajar)) {
        $u_res = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT nama_lengkap FROM users WHERE id = $id_ustadz"));
        $nama_pengajar = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $u_res['nama_lengkap'] ?? '');
    }

    $sql = "INSERT INTO halaqah (nama_halaqah, id_ustadz, nama_pengajar, keterangan) 
            VALUES ('$nama_halaqah', $id_ustadz, '$nama_pengajar', '$keterangan')";
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
        $new_id = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
        if ($id_ustadz !== 'NULL') {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET id_halaqah = $new_id WHERE id = $id_ustadz");
        }
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Lokal '$nama_halaqah' berhasil ditambahkan.";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal menambahkan lokal: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php");
    exit;
}

// === 3. EDIT KELAS / LOKAL (KHUSUS OPERATOR / PIMPINAN) ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['edit_kelas']) && $is_pengelola_penuh) {
    $id_kelas      = (int) $_POST['id_kelas'];
    $nama_halaqah  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_halaqah']));
    $id_ustadz     = !empty($_POST['id_ustadz']) ? (int) $_POST['id_ustadz'] : 'NULL';
    $nama_pengajar = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_pengajar'] ?? ''));
    $keterangan    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['keterangan']));

    if ($id_ustadz !== 'NULL' && empty($nama_pengajar)) {
        $u_res = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT nama_lengkap FROM users WHERE id = $id_ustadz"));
        $nama_pengajar = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $u_res['nama_lengkap'] ?? '');
    }

    $sql = "UPDATE halaqah SET 
                nama_halaqah = '$nama_halaqah', 
                id_ustadz = $id_ustadz, 
                nama_pengajar = '$nama_pengajar', 
                keterangan = '$keterangan' 
            WHERE id = $id_kelas";
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
        if ($id_ustadz !== 'NULL') {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET id_halaqah = $id_kelas WHERE id = $id_ustadz");
        }
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data Lokal '$nama_halaqah' berhasil diperbarui.";
    }
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php?id_kelas=$id_kelas");
    exit;
}

// === 4. HAPUS KELAS / LOKAL (KHUSUS OPERATOR / PIMPINAN) ===
if (isset($_GET['hapus_kelas']) && $is_pengelola_penuh) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_GET['hapus_kelas'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE santri SET id_halaqah = NULL WHERE id_halaqah = $id_hapus");
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM halaqah WHERE id = $id_hapus");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data lokal berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php");
    exit;
}

// === 5. MASUKKAN SANTRI KE KELAS INI ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['assign_santri'])) {
    $id_kelas = (int) $_POST['id_kelas'];
    if (!empty($_POST['id_santri_arr']) && is_array($_POST['id_santri_arr'])) {
        $ids = array_map('intval', $_POST['id_santri_arr']);
        $id_list = implode(',', $ids);
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE santri SET id_halaqah = $id_kelas WHERE id IN ($id_list)");
        $_SESSION['success'] = count($ids) . " santri berhasil ditempatkan di lokal ini.";
    }
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php?id_kelas=$id_kelas");
    exit;
}

// === 6. KELUARKAN SANTRI DARI KELAS ===
if (isset($_GET['keluarkan_santri']) && isset($_GET['id_kelas'])) {
    $id_s = (int) $_GET['keluarkan_santri'];
    $id_k = (int) $_GET['id_kelas'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE santri SET id_halaqah = NULL WHERE id = $id_s");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Santri berhasil dikeluarkan dari lokal.";
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php?id_kelas=$id_k");
    exit;
}

// =========================================================================
// FILTER DAFTAR KELAS BERDASARKAN AKTOR (OPERATOR/PIMPINAN vs USTADZ/AH)
// =========================================================================
$where_lokal = "";
if (!$is_pengelola_penuh) {
    // Ambil id_halaqah yang tercatat di tabel users untuk ustadz/ustadzah yang sedang login
    $u_login = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id_halaqah, nama_lengkap FROM users WHERE id = $id_user"));
    $id_halaqah_user = (int) ($u_login['id_halaqah'] ?? 0);
    $nama_ust_esc    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($u_login['nama_lengkap'] ?? $nama_user));

    // Tampilkan hanya lokal yang id_ustadz = user ini, ATAU id = id_halaqah di akun user, ATAU nama_pengajar sama dengan nama ustadz/ah
    $where_lokal = "WHERE (
                        h.id_ustadz = $id_user 
                        OR h.id = $id_halaqah_user 
                        OR (h.nama_pengajar IS NOT NULL AND h.nama_pengajar != '' AND LOWER(h.nama_pengajar) = LOWER('$nama_ust_esc'))
                    )";
}

$q_kelas = "SELECT h.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_user_ustadz, u.username as user_ustadz,
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM santri s WHERE s.id_halaqah = h.id) as jumlah_santri
            FROM halaqah h
            LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
            $where_lokal
            ORDER BY h.id ASC";
$kelas_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $q_kelas), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Hitung jumlah per kategori program untuk badge tab filter
$count_kat = ['all' => count($kelas_list), 'asrama' => 0, 'menginap' => 0, 'isya' => 0, 'maghrib' => 0];
$total_santri_terplot = 0;

foreach ($kelas_list as $kl) {
    $ket = $kl['keterangan'] ?? '';
    $total_santri_terplot += (int)$kl['jumlah_santri'];
    if (stripos($ket, 'Asrama') !== false) $count_kat['asrama']++;
    elseif (stripos($ket, 'Menginap') !== false) $count_kat['menginap']++;
    elseif (stripos($ket, 'Sebelum Maghrib') !== false) $count_kat['maghrib']++;
    else $count_kat['isya']++;
}

// Ambil semua Ustadz/Ustadzah untuk dropdown pilihan pengajar (khusus Operator/Pimpinan)
$ustadz_list = $is_pengelola_penuh 
    ? mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_lengkap, username FROM users WHERE role = 'ustadz' ORDER BY nama_lengkap ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC) 
    : [];

// Ambil semua santri untuk modal penempatan santri
$semua_santri = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT s.*, h.nama_halaqah FROM santri s LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id ORDER BY s.nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Tentukan Kelas yang sedang dipilih untuk dilihat detail santrinya
// Jika Ustadz/Ustadzah hanya mengampu 1 lokal, otomatis buka detail lokal tersebut!
$id_kelas_aktif = isset($_GET['id_kelas']) ? (int) $_GET['id_kelas'] : 0;
if ($id_kelas_aktif === 0 && !$is_pengelola_penuh && count($kelas_list) === 1) {
    $id_kelas_aktif = (int) $kelas_list[0]['id'];
}

$kelas_aktif = null;
$santri_di_kelas = [];

if ($id_kelas_aktif > 0) {
    foreach ($kelas_list as $kl) {
        if ((int)$kl['id'] === $id_kelas_aktif) {
            $kelas_aktif = $kl;
            break;
        }
    }
    if ($kelas_aktif) {
        $santri_di_kelas = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_halaqah = $id_kelas_aktif ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }
}

function styleProgramLokal($ket) {
    if (stripos($ket, 'Asrama') !== false) {
        return ['dot' => 'bg-amber-500', 'badge' => 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200/80', 'icon' => 'building-2'];
    } elseif (stripos($ket, 'Menginap') !== false) {
        return ['dot' => 'bg-blue-500', 'badge' => 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200/80', 'icon' => 'moon'];
    } elseif (stripos($ket, 'Sebelum Maghrib') !== false) {
        return ['dot' => 'bg-emerald-500', 'badge' => 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200/80', 'icon' => 'sun'];
    }
    return ['dot' => 'bg-indigo-500', 'badge' => 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border-indigo-200/80', 'icon' => 'clock'];
}

$page_title = "Kelas & Lokal Halaqah - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";

                        <i data-lucide="sliders-horizontal" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Pengaturan Lokal

                    </h3>

                        <button type="button" onclick="bukaModalAssignSantri()" class="bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 text-xs font-semibold px-4 py-2 rounded-lg transition inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm">
                            <i data-lucide="plus" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-600"></i> Pilih Santri Sekarang
                        </button>
                    </div>
                <?php else: ?>

<?php

startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: HANYA OPERATOR yang boleh mengakses halaman pengaturan hak akses ini
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'operator') {
    header("Location: dashboard.php");
    exit;
}

// === 1. SIMPAN PERUBAHAN MATRIKS HAK AKSES MENU ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['simpan_hak_akses'])) {
    $semua_menu = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, kode_menu FROM hak_akses_menu"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

    foreach ($semua_menu as $m) {
        $id_m = (int)$m['id'];
        // Operator selalu memiliki akses penuh (1) ke kelola_hak_akses
        $op   = ($m['kode_menu'] === 'kelola_hak_akses') ? 1 : (isset($_POST['akses_operator'][$id_m]) ? 1 : 0);
        $pimp = isset($_POST['akses_pimpinan'][$id_m]) ? 1 : 0;
        $ust  = isset($_POST['akses_ustadz'][$id_m]) ? 1 : 0;
        $ortu = isset($_POST['akses_ortu'][$id_m]) ? 1 : 0;

        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE hak_akses_menu SET 
                                akses_operator = $op,
                                akses_pimpinan = $pimp,
                                akses_ustadz = $ust,
                                akses_ortu = $ortu
                             WHERE id = $id_m");
    }

    $_SESSION['success'] = "Konfigurasi hak akses menu untuk seluruh Actor berhasil diperbarui secara real-time!";
    header("Location: kelola_hak_akses.php");
    exit;
}

// === 2. TAMBAH AKUN ACTOR BARU (OPERATOR / PIMPINAN / USTADZ) ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['tambah_akun_actor'])) {
    $nama_lengkap = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lengkap']));
    $username     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username']));
    $password     = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
    $role_baru    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['role_baru']);

    $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username' sudah terdaftar!";
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) VALUES ('$username', '$password', '$nama_lengkap', '$role_baru')");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Akun '$nama_lengkap' dengan role " . strtoupper($role_baru) . " berhasil dibuat!";
    }
    header("Location: kelola_hak_akses.php");
    exit;
}

// === 3. HAPUS AKUN PENGELOLA ===
if (isset($_GET['hapus_user'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int)$_GET['hapus_user'];
    if ($id_hapus !== (int)$_SESSION['user_id']) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $id_hapus");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Akun pengguna berhasil dihapus.";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Anda tidak dapat menghapus akun Operator yang sedang Anda gunakan login.";
    }
    header("Location: kelola_hak_akses.php");
    exit;
}

// Ambil seluruh daftar menu dikelompokkan per kategori
$menu_res = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM hak_akses_menu ORDER BY urutan ASC");
$menu_list = mysqli_fetch_all($menu_res, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$menu_by_kategori = [];
foreach ($menu_list as $m) {
    $menu_by_kategori[$m['kategori']][] = $m;
}

// Ambil daftar akun Operator & Pimpinan
$admin_users = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM users WHERE role IN ('operator', 'pimpinan') ORDER BY role ASC, nama_lengkap ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Statistik ringkas sistem untuk Operator
$stat_op   = (int)mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM users WHERE role='operator'"))['c'];
$stat_pimp = (int)mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM users WHERE role='pimpinan'"))['c'];
$stat_ust  = (int)mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM users WHERE role='ustadz'"))['c'];
$stat_ortu = (int)mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM users WHERE role='ortu'"))['c'];
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Manajemen Hak Akses Actor - Operator Sistem";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';

===== pages/pengguna/profil_saya.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_user = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role    = $_SESSION['role'];

// Ambil data user saat ini
$user_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT u.*, h.nama_halaqah 
                               FROM users u 
                               LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON (u.id_halaqah = h.id OR h.id_ustadz = u.id) 
                               WHERE u.id = $id_user LIMIT 1");
$user_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc($user_q);

// Jika role ortu, ambil data santri terkait untuk menampilkan/mengedit No. HP WhatsApp
$santri_ortu = null;
if ($role === 'ortu') {
    $s_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_user LIMIT 1");
    $santri_ortu = mysqli_fetch_assoc($s_q);
}

// === 1. PROSES UPDATE PROFIL ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['update_profil'])) {
    $nama_baru   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lengkap']));
    $username_br = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username']));
    
    // Cek apakah username dipakai orang lain
    $cek_u = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username_br' AND id != $id_user");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_u) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username_br' sudah digunakan oleh pengguna lain!";
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET nama_lengkap = '$nama_baru', username = '$username_br' WHERE id = $id_user");
        $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] = stripslashes($nama_baru);
        $_SESSION['username']     = stripslashes($username_br);

        // Jika ustadz/ah, sinkronkan juga nama_pengajar di tabel halaqah
        if ($role === 'ustadz') {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET nama_pengajar = '$nama_baru' WHERE id_ustadz = $id_user");
        }

        // Jika ortu, update juga nama_ortu & no_hp_ortu di tabel santri
        if ($role === 'ortu' && isset($_POST['no_hp_ortu'])) {
            $hp_baru = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['no_hp_ortu']));
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE santri SET nama_ortu = '$nama_baru', no_hp_ortu = '$hp_baru' WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_user");
        }

        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data profil Anda berhasil diperbarui!";
    }
    header("Location: profil_saya.php");
    exit;
}

// === 2. PROSES GANTI PASSWORD ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['ganti_password'])) {
    $pass_lama       = $_POST['password_lama'] ?? '';
    $pass_baru       = $_POST['password_baru'] ?? '';
    $pass_konfirmasi = $_POST['konfirmasi_password'] ?? '';

    if (!password_verify($pass_lama, $user_data['password'])) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Password saat ini yang Anda masukkan salah!";
    } elseif (strlen($pass_baru) < 5) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Password baru minimal harus terdiri dari 5 karakter!";
    } elseif ($pass_baru !== $pass_konfirmasi) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Konfirmasi password baru tidak cocok!";
    } else {
        $hash_baru = password_hash($pass_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET password = '$hash_baru' WHERE id = $id_user");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Password akun Anda berhasil diubah! Gunakan password baru saat login berikutnya.";
    }
    header("Location: profil_saya.php");
    exit;
}

$label_role = ucfirst($role);
if ($role === 'ustadz') {
    $label_role = isPengajarUstadzah($conn, $id_user) ? 'Ustadzah (Pengajar Putri)' : 'Ustadz (Pengajar Putra)';
} elseif ($role === 'ortu') {
    $label_role = 'Wali Santri / Orang Tua';
} elseif ($role === 'operator') {
    $label_role = 'Operator Utama Sistem';
}
?>

            </h1>
            <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">Kelola informasi identitas akun Anda dan perbarui kata sandi secara berkala untuk menjaga keamanan.</p>

                <i data-lucide="alert-circle" class="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 shrink-0"></i>
                <span><?= $_SESSION['error']; unset($_SESSION['error']); ?></span>
            </div>

===== pages/pengguna/ustadz.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Operator, Pimpinan, dan Ustadz (dikontrol juga dari hak_akses_menu)
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'pimpinan', 'ustadz'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom jenis_kelamin di tabel users jika belum ada
$cek_kolom = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM users LIKE 'jenis_kelamin'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN jenis_kelamin ENUM('L','P') DEFAULT 'L' AFTER nama_lengkap");
}

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom id_halaqah di tabel users jika belum ada
$cek_kolom_h = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM users LIKE 'id_halaqah'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom_h) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN id_halaqah INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER role");
}

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom nama_pengajar di tabel halaqah jika belum ada
$cek_kolom_np = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM halaqah LIKE 'nama_pengajar'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom_np) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE halaqah ADD COLUMN nama_pengajar VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL AFTER id_ustadz");
}

// === 1. PROSES TAMBAH USTADZ / USTADZAH ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['tambah_ustadz'])) {
    $nama_lengkap  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lengkap']));
    $jenis_kelamin = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $username      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username']));
    $password      = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
    $nama_lokal    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lokal']));
    $sebutan       = ($jenis_kelamin === 'P') ? 'Ustadzah' : 'Ustadz';

    // Cek apakah Lokal/Halaqah sudah ada, jika belum buat baru
    $cek_lokal = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM halaqah WHERE nama_halaqah = '$nama_lokal'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_lokal) > 0) {
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_lokal)['id'];
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO halaqah (nama_halaqah) VALUES ('$nama_lokal')");
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
    }

    // Cek duplikasi Username
    $cek_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_user) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username' sudah digunakan! Silakan gunakan username lain.";
    } else {
        $sql = "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, jenis_kelamin, role, id_halaqah) 
                VALUES ('$username', '$password', '$nama_lengkap', '$jenis_kelamin', 'ustadz', $id_halaqah)";
        if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
            $id_user = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET id_ustadz = $id_user, nama_pengajar = '$nama_lengkap' WHERE id = $id_halaqah");
            $_SESSION['success'] = "$sebutan '$nama_lengkap' berhasil ditambahkan ke $nama_lokal!";
        } else {
            $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal menyimpan data: " . mysqli_error($conn);
        }
    }
    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}

// === 2. PROSES EDIT USTADZ / USTADZAH ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['edit_ustadz'])) {
    $id_user       = (int) $_POST['id_user'];
    $nama_lengkap  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lengkap']));
    $jenis_kelamin = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $username      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username']));
    $nama_lokal    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lokal']));
    $password_baru = trim($_POST['password_baru'] ?? '');
    $sebutan       = ($jenis_kelamin === 'P') ? 'Ustadzah' : 'Ustadz';

    // Cek username bentrok dengan user lain
    $cek_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username' AND id != $id_user");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_user) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username' sudah dipakai pengguna lain!";
        header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
        exit;
    }

    // Cek / Buat Lokal Mengajar
    $cek_lokal = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM halaqah WHERE nama_halaqah = '$nama_lokal'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_lokal) > 0) {
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_lokal)['id'];
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO halaqah (nama_halaqah) VALUES ('$nama_lokal')");
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
    }

    $pass_sql = "";
    if (!empty($password_baru)) {
        $hash = password_hash($password_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        $pass_sql = ", password = '$hash'";
    }

    $sql_update = "UPDATE users SET 
                    nama_lengkap = '$nama_lengkap',
                    jenis_kelamin = '$jenis_kelamin',
                    username = '$username',
                    id_halaqah = $id_halaqah
                    $pass_sql
                   WHERE id = $id_user AND role = 'ustadz'";

    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql_update)) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET id_ustadz = $id_user, nama_pengajar = '$nama_lengkap' WHERE id = $id_halaqah");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data $sebutan '$nama_lengkap' berhasil diperbarui!";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal memperbarui data.";
    }
    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}

// === 3. PROSES HAPUS USTADZ / USTADZAH ===
if (isset($_GET['hapus'])) {
    $id = (int) $_GET['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET id_ustadz = NULL WHERE id_ustadz = $id");
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $id AND role = 'ustadz'");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data pengajar (Ustadz/Ustadzah) berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}

// Ambil data seluruh pengajar beserta nama lokalnya
$query_ustadz = "SELECT u.*, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM users u 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON (u.id_halaqah = h.id OR h.id_ustadz = u.id) 
                 WHERE u.role = 'ustadz' 
                 ORDER BY u.nama_lengkap ASC";
$res_ustadz = mysqli_query($conn, $query_ustadz);
$ustadz_list = $res_ustadz ? mysqli_fetch_all($res_ustadz, MYSQLI_ASSOC) : [];

// Hitung Statistik Ustadz & Ustadzah
$total_pengajar = count($ustadz_list);
$total_ustadz   = 0;
$total_ustadzah = 0;
foreach ($ustadz_list as $u) {
    // Deteksi otomatis: jika jenis_kelamin 'P' atau namanya mengandung kata 'ustadzah'
    $is_putri = (($u['jenis_kelamin'] ?? 'L') === 'P' || stripos($u['nama_lengkap'], 'ustadzah') !== false);
    if ($is_putri) {
        $total_ustadzah++;
    } else {
        $total_ustadz++;
    }
}
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Kelola Ustadz & Ustadzah - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <!-- Header Halaman -->
        <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-end gap-4 mb-8">
            <div>
                <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div class="p-2.5 bg-indigo-100 rounded-xl">
                        <i data-lucide="users" class="w-7 h-7 text-indigo-600"></i>
                    </div>
                    Kelola Ustadz & Ustadzah
                </h1>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">Manajemen akun pengajar tahfidz (Ustadz & Ustadzah) beserta penempatan lokal halaqah.</p>
            </div>
            <button type="button" onclick="toggleFormPengajar()" class="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-semibold py-2.5 px-5 rounded-xl transition shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <i data-lucide="user-plus" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                <span>Tambah Ustadz / Ustadzah</span>
            </button>
        </div>

        <!-- Notifikasi -->
        <?php if (isset($_SESSION['success'])): ?>
            <div class="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl mb-6 text-sm flex items-center gap-3 shadow-sm">
                <i data-lucide="check-circle-2" class="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0"></i>
                <span class="font-medium"><?= $_SESSION['success']; unset($_SESSION['success']); ?></span>
            </div>
        <?php endif; ?>

        <?php if (isset($_SESSION['error'])): ?>
            <div class="bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl mb-6 text-sm flex items-center gap-3 shadow-sm">
                <i data-lucide="alert-circle" class="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 shrink-0"></i>
                <span class="font-medium"><?= $_SESSION['error']; unset($_SESSION['error']); ?></span>
            </div>
        <?php endif; ?>

        <!-- Kartu Statistik Pengajar -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-8">
            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Total Pengajar</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $total_pengajar ?></p>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Pembimbing Halaqah Aktif</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="users" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Ustadz (Pengajar Putra)</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $total_ustadz ?></p>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Pembimbing Santriwan</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="user-check" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-teal-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Ustadzah (Pengajar Putri)</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $total_ustadzah ?></p>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Pembimbing Santriwati</p>

===== pages/rapor/input.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Daftar 37 Surat Juz 30 sesuai urutan di Excel Surau
$surat_juz_30 = [
    "An- Naba'", "An- Nazi'at", "Abasa", "At- Takwir", "Al- Infithar",
    "Al- Muthaffifin", "Al- Insyiqaq", "Al- Buruj", "Ath- Thariq", "Al-A'la",
    "Al- Ghasyiyyah", "Al- Fajr", "Al- Balad", "Asy- Syams", "Al- Lail",
    "Ad- Dhuha", "Asy- Syarth", "At- Tiin", "Al-'Alaq", "Al-Qadr",
    "Al- Bayyinah", "Az- Zalzalah", "Al- 'Adiyat", "Al- Qari'ah", "At- Takatsur",
    "Al- 'Ashr", "Al- Humazah", "Al- Fiil", "Quraisy", "Al- Ma'un",
    "Al- Kautsar", "Al- Kafirun", "An- Nashr", "Al- Lahab", "Al- Ikhlash",
    "Al- Falaq", "An- Naas"
];

// Ambil daftar santri beserta Lokal/Kelas & Ustadz Pengampunya
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $_SESSION['user_id'], $_SESSION['role'], 's');
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, s.jenis_kelamin, s.id_halaqah, 
                        h.nama_halaqah, h.keterangan as ket_lokal,
                        COALESCE(u.nama_lengkap, h.nama_pengajar) as pengajar_lokal
                 FROM santri s
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id
                 LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
                 $where_binaan
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_res = mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($santri_res, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$lokal_res = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_halaqah, keterangan FROM halaqah ORDER BY id ASC");
$lokal_list = $lokal_res ? mysqli_fetch_all($lokal_res, MYSQLI_ASSOC) : [];

$id_santri    = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($santri_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$juz          = isset($_GET['juz']) ? (int) $_GET['juz'] : 30; // Default Juz 30
$semester     = isset($_GET['semester']) ? $_GET['semester'] : '1 (Satu)';
$tahun_ajaran = isset($_GET['tahun_ajaran']) ? $_GET['tahun_ajaran'] : date('Y');

$santri_aktif = null;
foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
    if ((int)$s['id'] === $id_santri) {
        $santri_aktif = $s;
        break;
    }
}

// Ambil Data Rapor jika sudah pernah disimpan
$header_data = null;
$detail_data = [];
$absensi_db  = ['S' => 0, 'I' => 0, 'A' => 0];

if ($santri_aktif) {
    $sem_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $semester);
    $thn_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $tahun_ajaran);
    
    // Cek tabel rapor_tahfidz
    $cek_tbl = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW TABLES LIKE 'rapor_tahfidz'");
    if ($cek_tbl && mysqli_num_rows($cek_tbl) > 0) {
        $header_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM rapor_tahfidz WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND juz = $juz AND semester = '$sem_esc' AND tahun_ajaran = '$thn_esc' LIMIT 1");
        if ($header_q) {
            $header_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc($header_q);
            if ($header_data) {
                $detail_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM rapor_tahfidz_detail WHERE id_rapor = {$header_data['id']} ORDER BY urutan ASC");
                $detail_data = mysqli_fetch_all($detail_q, MYSQLI_ASSOC);
            }
        }
    }

    // Hitung otomatis Sakit, Izin, Alfa dari tabel absensi sebagai nilai default
    $q_abs = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT status, COUNT(*) as jml FROM absensi WHERE id_santri = $id_santri GROUP BY status");
    if ($q_abs) {
        while ($ab = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_abs)) {
            $in = strtoupper(substr(trim($ab['status']), 0, 1));
            if (isset($absensi_db[$in])) $absensi_db[$in] += (int)$ab['jml'];
        }
    }
}

// Kelas Otomatis dari Lokal Halaqah Santri
if (!empty($santri_aktif['nama_halaqah'])) {
    $default_kelas = $santri_aktif['nama_halaqah'];
} elseif (!empty($header_data['kelas'])) {
    $default_kelas = $header_data['kelas'];
} else {
    $default_kelas = ($santri_aktif && $santri_aktif['jenis_kelamin'] === 'P') ? 'Khusus Perempuan' : 'Khusus Laki-Laki';
}

$default_judul = $header_data['judul_materi'] ?? "Materi Tahfidz (Juz $juz)";
$default_wali  = $header_data['wali_kelas'] ?? (($santri_aktif['pengajar_lokal'] ?? '') ?: 'Ust. Hadi Latif, M.Pd');

// Siapkan daftar baris materi default jika belum pernah disimpan di DB
$baris_form = [];
if (count($detail_data) > 0) {
    foreach ($detail_data as $d) {
        $baris_form[] = ['materi' => $d['nama_materi'], 'nilai' => $d['nilai']];
    }
} else {
    if ($juz === 30) {
        // Khusus Juz 30: Otomatis 37 Surat
        foreach ($surat_juz_30 as $nama_srt) {
            $baris_form[] = ['materi' => $nama_srt, 'nilai' => ''];
        }
    } else {
        // Selain Juz 30 (Juz 1 - 29): Otomatis 20 Pojok sesuai halaman Mushaf
        for ($i = 1; $i <= 20; $i++) {
            $hal = ($juz - 1) * 20 + $i + 1;
            $label_hal = ($juz === 1 && $i === 1) ? "Al-fatihah + Halaman 2" : "Halaman $hal";
            $baris_form[] = ['materi' => $label_hal, 'nilai' => ''];
        }
    }
}
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Input Leger Rapor - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <!-- HEADER -->
        <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-start gap-4 mb-6">
            <div>
                <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <div class="p-2 bg-indigo-100 rounded-xl"><i data-lucide="table-2" class="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600"></i></div>
                    Input Leger Nilai Rapor
                </h1>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
