===== pages/rapor/rekap_bulanan.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$bulan_dipilih = isset($_GET['bulan']) ? str_pad((int)$_GET['bulan'], 2, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT) : date('m');
$tahun_dipilih = isset($_GET['tahun']) ? (int)$_GET['tahun'] : (int)date('Y');
$id_lokal      = isset($_GET['id_lokal']) ? (int)$_GET['id_lokal'] : 0;
$periode       = $tahun_dipilih . '-' . $bulan_dipilih;

$nama_bulan = [
    '01' => 'Januari', '02' => 'Februari', '03' => 'Maret', '04' => 'April',
    '05' => 'Mei', '06' => 'Juni', '07' => 'Juli', '08' => 'Agustus',
    '09' => 'September', '10' => 'Oktober', '11' => 'November', '12' => 'Desember'
];

// Ambil daftar lokal untuk filter
$lokal_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_halaqah FROM halaqah ORDER BY id ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
$nama_lokal_terpilih = "Semua Kelas / Lokal";
foreach ($lokal_list as $lk) {
    if ((int)$lk['id'] === $id_lokal) {
        $nama_lokal_terpilih = $lk['nama_halaqah'];
        break;
    }
}

$where_lokal = $id_lokal > 0 ? "WHERE s.id_halaqah = $id_lokal" : "";

// Query akurat menggunakan Subquery terpisah untuk Hafalan (h) dan Absensi (a)
// agar TIDAK terjadi duplikasi perkalian baris (Cartesian Product)
$sql_santri = "SELECT 
                  s.id, s.nama, s.nis, hl.nama_halaqah,
                  COALESCE(h.total_ayat, 0) as total_ayat,
                  COALESCE(h.total_murajaah, 0) as total_murajaah,
                  COALESCE(h.total_tasmi, 0) as total_tasmi,
                  COALESCE(a.hadir, 0) as hadir,
                  COALESCE(a.sakit, 0) as sakit,
                  COALESCE(a.izin, 0) as izin,
                  COALESCE(a.alpa, 0) as alpa
               FROM santri s
               LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
               LEFT JOIN (
                   SELECT id_santri,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END) as total_ayat,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as total_murajaah,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as total_tasmi
                   FROM hafalan
                   WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$periode'
                   GROUP BY id_santri
               ) h ON s.id = h.id_santri
               LEFT JOIN (
                   SELECT id_santri,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(LEFT(TRIM(status), 1)) = 'H' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as hadir,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(LEFT(TRIM(status), 1)) = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as sakit,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(LEFT(TRIM(status), 1)) = 'I' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as izin,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(LEFT(TRIM(status), 1)) = 'A' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as alpa
                   FROM absensi
                   WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$periode'
                   GROUP BY id_santri
               ) a ON s.id = a.id_santri
               $where_lokal
               ORDER BY s.nama ASC";

$data_santri = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $sql_santri), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
?>

===== pages/rapor/santri.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$role    = $_SESSION['role'];
$user_id = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// Fungsi konversi angka ke kata Terbilang Bahasa Indonesia
function terbilang_indonesia($angka) {
    $angka = abs((int) round($angka));
    $baca  = ["", "Satu", "Dua", "Tiga", "Empat", "Lima", "Enam", "Tujuh", "Delapan", "Sembilan", "Sepuluh", "Sebelas"];
    if ($angka === 0) return "Nol";
    
    $terbilang = "";
    if ($angka < 12) {
        $terbilang = " " . $baca[$angka];
    } elseif ($angka < 20) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia($angka - 10) . " Belas";
    } elseif ($angka < 100) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia((int)($angka / 10)) . " Puluh" . ($angka % 10 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka % 10) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 200) {
        $terbilang = " Seratus" . ($angka - 100 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka - 100) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 1000) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia((int)($angka / 100)) . " Ratus" . ($angka % 100 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka % 100) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 2000) {
        $terbilang = " Seribu" . ($angka - 1000 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka - 1000) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 1000000) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia((int)($angka / 1000)) . " Ribu" . ($angka % 1000 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka % 1000) : "");
    }
    return trim($terbilang);
}

// Daftar 37 Surat Juz 30 untuk fallback tampilan jika belum diinput
$default_surat_juz30 = [
    "An- Naba'", "An- Nazi'at", "Abasa", "At- Takwir", "Al- Infithar",
    "Al- Muthaffifin", "Al- Insyiqaq", "Al- Buruj", "Ath- Thariq", "Al-A'la",
    "Al- Ghasyiyyah", "Al- Fajr", "Al- Balad", "Asy- Syams", "Al- Lail",
    "Ad- Dhuha", "Asy- Syarth", "At- Tiin", "Al-'Alaq", "Al-Qadr",
    "Al- Bayyinah", "Az- Zalzalah", "Al- 'Adiyat", "Al- Qari'ah", "At- Takatsur",
    "Al- 'Ashr", "Al- Humazah", "Al- Fiil", "Quraisy", "Al- Ma'un",
    "Al- Kautsar", "Al- Kafirun", "An- Nashr", "Al- Lahab", "Al- Ikhlash",
    "Al- Falaq", "An- Naas"
];

// Ambil daftar santri beserta Lokal/Kelas & Ustadz Pengampunya
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';
if ($role === 'ortu') {
    $where_filter = "WHERE s.id_user_ortu = $user_id";
} else {
    $where_filter = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $user_id, $role, 's');
}

$santri_query = "SELECT s.*, h.nama_halaqah, h.keterangan as ket_lokal,
                        COALESCE(u.nama_lengkap, h.nama_pengajar) as pengajar_lokal
                 FROM santri s
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id
                 LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
                 $where_filter
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_res = mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($santri_res, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$id_santri    = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($santri_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
// Dukungan parameter mode_juz ('30', '1-2', '2-3', atau '1', '2', dst.)
$mode_juz     = $_GET['mode_juz'] ?? ($_GET['juz'] ?? '30');
$semester     = isset($_GET['semester']) ? $_GET['semester'] : '1 (Satu)';
$tahun_ajaran = isset($_GET['tahun_ajaran']) ? $_GET['tahun_ajaran'] : date('Y');

$santri_aktif = null;
foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
    if ((int)$s['id'] === $id_santri) {
        $santri_aktif = $s;
        break;
    }
}

// Parse daftar Juz yang akan ditampilkan (Bisa 1 Juz seperti [30] atau 2 Juz seperti [1, 2])
$juz_tampil_list = [];
if (strpos($mode_juz, '-') !== false) {
    $parts = explode('-', $mode_juz);
    $juz_tampil_list[] = (int) $parts[0];
    $juz_tampil_list[] = (int) $parts[1];
} else {
    $juz_tampil_list[] = (int) $mode_juz;
}

// Fungsi ambil data 1 Juz dari database (beserta fallback 37 surat atau 20 pojok)
function ambilDataRaporJuz($conn, $id_santri, $juz_num, $semester, $tahun_ajaran, $default_surat_juz30) {
    $sem_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $semester);
    $thn_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $tahun_ajaran);
    
    $header = null;
    $details = [];
    $cek_tbl = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW TABLES LIKE 'rapor_tahfidz'");
    if ($cek_tbl && mysqli_num_rows($cek_tbl) > 0 && $id_santri > 0) {
        $q_h = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM rapor_tahfidz WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND juz = $juz_num AND semester = '$sem_esc' AND tahun_ajaran = '$thn_esc' LIMIT 1");
        if ($q_h) {
            $header = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_h);
            if ($header) {
                $q_d = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM rapor_tahfidz_detail WHERE id_rapor = {$header['id']} ORDER BY urutan ASC");
                $details = mysqli_fetch_all($q_d, MYSQLI_ASSOC);
            }
        }
    }

    // Jika belum ada detail di DB, bangun kerangka baris default (37 surat utk Juz 30, 20 pojok utk lainnya)
    if (empty($details)) {
        if ($juz_num === 30) {
            foreach ($default_surat_juz30 as $idx => $srt) {
                $details[] = ['urutan' => $idx + 1, 'nama_materi' => $srt, 'nilai' => null];
            }
        } else {
            for ($i = 1; $i <= 20; $i++) {
                $hal = ($juz_num - 1) * 20 + $i + 1;
                $materi = ($juz_num === 1 && $i === 1) ? "Al-fatihah + Halaman 2" : "Halaman $hal";
                $details[] = ['urutan' => $i, 'nama_materi' => $materi, 'nilai' => null];
            }
        }
    }

    return ['header' => $header, 'details' => $details];
}

$rapor_per_juz = [];
$header_utama  = null;
foreach ($juz_tampil_list as $jz) {
    $data_jz = ambilDataRaporJuz($conn, $santri_aktif['id'] ?? 0, $jz, $semester, $tahun_ajaran, $default_surat_juz30);
    $rapor_per_juz[$jz] = $data_jz;
    if (!$header_utama && $data_jz['header']) {
        $header_utama = $data_jz['header'];
    }
}

// Kelas Otomatis dari Lokal Halaqah Santri
if (!empty($santri_aktif['nama_halaqah'])) {
    $kelas_tampil = $santri_aktif['nama_halaqah'];
} elseif (!empty($header_utama['kelas'])) {
    $kelas_tampil = $header_utama['kelas'];
} else {
    $kelas_tampil = ($santri_aktif && $santri_aktif['jenis_kelamin'] === 'P') ? 'Khusus Perempuan' : 'Khusus Laki-Laki';
}

$wali_kelas_tampil = !empty($header_utama['wali_kelas']) ? $header_utama['wali_kelas'] : (!empty($santri_aktif['pengajar_lokal']) ? $santri_aktif['pengajar_lokal'] : 'Ust. Hadi Latif, M.Pd');
$kepala_stq_tampil = !empty($header_utama['kepala_stq']) ? $header_utama['kepala_stq'] : 'Ust. Aipda Syaiful Hendra';
$tempat_tampil     = !empty($header_utama['tempat_cetak']) ? $header_utama['tempat_cetak'] : 'Jalikur Patanangan';
$tanggal_tampil    = !empty($header_utama['tanggal_cetak']) ? $header_utama['tanggal_cetak'] : date('d F Y');
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Rapor Tahfidz (Juz " . htmlspecialchars($mode_juz) . ") - " . htmlspecialchars($santri_aktif['nama'] ?? 'Santri');
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-100 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <!-- TOOLBAR FILTER & PILIHAN MODE RAPOR -->
        <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-4 mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4 no-print">
            <form method="GET" action="rapor_santri.php" class="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1">Santri</label>
                    <select name="id_santri" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold">
                        <?php foreach ($santri_list as $s): ?>
                            <option value="<?= $s['id'] ?>" <?= (int)$s['id'] === $id_santri ? 'selected' : '' ?>>
                                <?= htmlspecialchars($s['nama']) ?> (<?= htmlspecialchars($s['nama_halaqah'] ?: 'Tanpa Lokal') ?>)
                            </option>
                        <?php endforeach; ?>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1">Pilih Format / Juz Rapor</label>
                    <select name="mode_juz" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-300 text-indigo-900 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold">
                        <option value="30" <?= $mode_juz === '30' ? 'selected' : '' ?>>★ Rapor Khusus Juz 30 (37 Surat)</option>
                        <optgroup label="Rapor Gabungan 2 Juz (2 Tabel / Halaman)">
                            <?php for ($g = 1; $g <= 29; $g++): 
                                $pair = $g . '-' . ($g + 1);
                            ?>
                                <option value="<?= $pair ?>" <?= $mode_juz === $pair ? 'selected' : '' ?>>Juz <?= $pair ?> (20 Pojok + 20 Pojok)</option>

                            <?php endfor; ?>

                    <?php 
                    $total_nilai = 0;
                    $jml_terisi  = 0;

                        $has_val = ($raw_val !== null && $raw_val !== '');
                        $angka   = $has_val ? (int) $raw_val : null;

                        if ($has_val) {
                            $total_nilai += $angka;
                            $jml_terisi++;
                            // Rumus Excel Surau: >=90 = A (Baik), >=85 = B (Diterima), <=84 = C (Gagal)
                            if ($angka >= 90) {
                                $prediket = 'A'; $ket = 'Baik';
                            } elseif ($angka >= 85) {
                                $prediket = 'B'; $ket = 'Diterima';
                            } else {
                                $prediket = 'C'; $ket = 'Gagal';
                            }
                        } else {
                            $prediket = ''; $ket = '';

                        <td style="text-align: center;"><?= $ket ?></td>
                    </tr>

                        <td colspan="2" style="text-align: center; font-style: italic; font-size: 11px;"><?= $terbilang_rata ?></td>
                    </tr>
                </tbody>
            </table>
            <?php endforeach; ?>

                $jml_ujian = array_sum($arr_ujian);
                $rata_ujian = count($arr_ujian) > 0 ? round($jml_ujian / 5, 1) : 0;
            ?>
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 20px; margin-top: 8px; margin-bottom: 12px;">
                <!-- Tabel Ujian Lintas Juz -->

                            <tr><td>Juz 3</td><td style="text-align: center;"><?= $u3 ?? '-' ?></td></tr>
                            <tr><td>Juz 4</td><td style="text-align: center;"><?= $u4 ?? '-' ?></td></tr>
                            <tr style="font-weight: bold;"><td>JUMLAH</td><td style="text-align: center;"><?= $jml_ujian > 0 ? $jml_ujian : '-' ?></td></tr>
                            <tr style="font-weight: bold;"><td>RATA-RATA</td><td style="text-align: center;"><?= $rata_ujian > 0 ? $rata_ujian : '-' ?></td></tr>
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <!-- Nilai Tahsin, Naik ke Juz & Remedi -->

                        <tr>

===== pages/santri/index.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Ustadz & Pimpinan dapat mengakses
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: dashboard.php");
    exit;
}

$query = "SELECT santri.*, users.username as username_ortu, halaqah.nama_halaqah, halaqah.keterangan as ket_halaqah 
          FROM santri 
          LEFT JOIN users ON santri.id_user_ortu = users.id 
          LEFT JOIN halaqah ON santri.id_halaqah = halaqah.id 
          ORDER BY santri.nama ASC";
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Ambil daftar lokal untuk dropdown di kelola_santri.php
$lokal_opsi = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_halaqah, keterangan FROM halaqah ORDER BY id ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Hitung Statistik Santri
$total_santri = count($santri_list);
$total_putra  = 0;
$total_putri  = 0;
foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
    if ($s['jenis_kelamin'] === 'L') $total_putra++;
    if ($s['jenis_kelamin'] === 'P') $total_putri++;
}
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Kelola Santri - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <!-- Header Halaman -->
        <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-end gap-4 mb-8">
            <div>
                <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div class="p-2.5 bg-indigo-100 rounded-xl">
                        <i data-lucide="graduation-cap" class="w-7 h-7 text-indigo-600"></i>
                    </div>
                    Kelola Data Santri
                </h1>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">Manajemen data induk santri binaan beserta integrasi akun portal orang tua.</p>
            </div>
            <button type="button" onclick="toggleFormSantri()" id="btnToggleForm" class="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-semibold py-2.5 px-5 rounded-xl transition shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <i data-lucide="user-plus" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                <span>Tambah Santri Baru</span>
            </button>
        </div>

        <!-- Notifikasi Sukses & Error -->
        <?php if (isset($_SESSION['success'])): ?>
            <div class="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl mb-6 text-sm flex items-center justify-between shadow-sm">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <i data-lucide="check-circle-2" class="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0"></i>
                    <span class="font-medium"><?= $_SESSION['success']; unset($_SESSION['success']); ?></span>
                </div>
            </div>
        <?php endif; ?>

        <?php if (isset($_SESSION['error'])): ?>
            <div class="bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl mb-6 text-sm flex items-center justify-between shadow-sm">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <i data-lucide="alert-circle" class="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 shrink-0"></i>
                    <span class="font-medium"><?= $_SESSION['error']; unset($_SESSION['error']); ?></span>
                </div>
            </div>
        <?php endif; ?>

        <!-- Kartu Statistik Ringkas -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-8">
            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Total Santri Aktif</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $total_santri ?></p>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Terdaftar dalam sistem</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="users" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-blue-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Santriwan (Putra)</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $total_putra ?></p>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1"><?= $total_santri > 0 ? round(($total_putra / $total_santri) * 100) : 0 ?>% dari total santri</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="user-check" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-pink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Santriwati (Putri)</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $total_putri ?></p>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1"><?= $total_santri > 0 ? round(($total_putri / $total_santri) * 100) : 0 ?>% dari total santri</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-pink-50 text-pink-600 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center">

            <div class="px-6 py-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-indigo-700 text-white flex justify-between items-center">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <i data-lucide="user-plus" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
                    <h3 class="text-base font-semibold">Formulir Pendaftaran Santri & Akun Wali</h3>
                </div>
                <button type="button" onclick="toggleFormSantri()" class="text-indigo-200 hover:text-white transition">
                    <i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
                </button>

===== pages/santri/progres.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ustadz = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role      = $_SESSION['role'];
$nama_user = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] ?? '';
$is_pengelola_penuh = in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan']);

// 1. Pastikan kolom juz & pojok tersedia di tabel hafalan agar tidak pernah error
$cek_juz = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM hafalan LIKE 'juz'");
if ($cek_juz && mysqli_num_rows($cek_juz) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `juz` INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `jenis`");
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `pojok` VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `juz`");
}

// 2. Filter santri sesuai lokal binaan jika yang login adalah Ustadz/Ustadzah
$where_santri = "";
$tampil_semua = isset($_GET['semua']) && $_GET['semua'] == '1';

if (!$is_pengelola_penuh && !$tampil_semua) {
    $u_info = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id_halaqah, nama_lengkap FROM users WHERE id = $id_ustadz"));
    $id_h_user = (int) ($u_info['id_halaqah'] ?? 0);
    $nm_esc    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($u_info['nama_lengkap'] ?? $nama_user));
    
    $q_lokal_ust = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM halaqah WHERE id_ustadz = $id_ustadz OR id = $id_h_user OR (nama_pengajar IS NOT NULL AND nama_pengajar != '' AND LOWER(nama_pengajar) = LOWER('$nm_esc'))");
    $lokal_ids = [];
    if ($q_lokal_ust) {
        while ($lk = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_lokal_ust)) {
            $lokal_ids[] = (int)$lk['id'];
        }
    }

    if (!empty($lokal_ids)) {
        $list_id_str = implode(',', $lokal_ids);
        $where_santri = "WHERE (s.id_halaqah IN ($list_id_str) OR s.id_ustadz = $id_ustadz)";
    } else {
        // Jika ustadz belum ditautkan ke lokal manapun, tampilkan santri yang pernah diinputnya atau semua santri sebagai fallback
        $where_santri = "";
    }
}

$q_santri = "SELECT s.*, h.nama_halaqah, h.keterangan as ket_lokal 
             FROM santri s 
             LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
             $where_santri 
             ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$res_santri  = mysqli_query($conn, $q_santri);
$santri_list = $res_santri ? mysqli_fetch_all($res_santri, MYSQLI_ASSOC) : [];

// 3. Perbaikan Pemilihan Santri Aktif (Selalu Fallback ke Santri Pertama jika ID tidak cocok/kosong)
$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : 0;
$santri_aktif = null;

if (!empty($santri_list)) {
    foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
        if ((int)$s['id'] === $id_santri_pilih) {
            $santri_aktif = $s;
            break;
        }
    }
    // Jika ID di URL kosong atau tidak ditemukan di daftar, otomatis pilih santri urutan pertama!
    if (!$santri_aktif) {
        $santri_aktif = $santri_list[0];
        $id_santri_pilih = (int) $santri_aktif['id'];
    }
}

$riwayat_hafalan = [];
$chart_labels    = [];
$chart_ziyadah   = [];
$stat            = ['ziyadah' => 0, 'murajaah' => 0, 'tasmi' => 0];
$target_aktif    = null;

if ($santri_aktif) {
    $id_s = (int) $santri_aktif['id'];
    
    // Statistik Total Santri Ini
    $q_st = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT jenis, COUNT(*) as jml, COALESCE(SUM(jumlah_ayat), 0) as tot_ayat FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_s GROUP BY jenis");
    if ($q_st) {
        while ($st = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_st)) {
            if ($st['jenis'] === 'ziyadah')  $stat['ziyadah']  = (int)$st['tot_ayat'];
            if ($st['jenis'] === 'murajaah') $stat['murajaah'] = (int)$st['jml'];
            if ($st['jenis'] === 'tasmi')    $stat['tasmi']    = (int)$st['jml'];
        }
    }

    // Target Hafalan Santri Ini
    $q_tgt = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_s ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1");
    if ($q_tgt && mysqli_num_rows($q_tgt) > 0) {
        $target_aktif = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_tgt);
    }

    // Riwayat Lengkap Setoran
    $q_riw = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT h.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz FROM hafalan h LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id WHERE h.id_santri = $id_s ORDER BY h.tanggal DESC, h.id DESC");
    if ($q_riw) {
        $riwayat_hafalan = mysqli_fetch_all($q_riw, MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }

    // Grafik 6 Bulan Terakhir
    for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
        $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $q_z = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(jumlah_ayat), 0) as z FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_s AND jenis = 'ziyadah' AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'");
        $chart_ziyadah[] = $q_z ? (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_z)['z'] : 0;
    }
}

===== pages/santri/target.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) { 
    header("Location: login.php"); 
    exit; 
}

$id_ustadz = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role      = $_SESSION['role'];

// Pastikan seluruh kolom yang dibutuhkan ada di tabel target_hafalan (auto-migrasi)
$kolom_target = [
    'target_ayat_per_hari'     => "INT(11) DEFAULT NULL",
    'target_surat'             => "VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL",
    'total_ayat_target'        => "INT(11) DEFAULT NULL",
    'tanggal_estimasi_selesai' => "DATE DEFAULT NULL",
    'keterangan'               => "TEXT DEFAULT NULL",
];
foreach ($kolom_target as $nama_kol => $definisi) {
    $cek_kol = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM target_hafalan LIKE '$nama_kol'");
    if ($cek_kol && mysqli_num_rows($cek_kol) === 0) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE target_hafalan ADD COLUMN `$nama_kol` $definisi");
    }
}

// Proses Simpan / Update Target
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $id_santri    = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $target_ayat  = max(1, (int) $_POST['target_ayat']);
    $target_surat = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['target_surat']));
    $total_ayat   = max(1, (int) $_POST['total_ayat']);
    $tgl_mulai    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal_mulai']);
    $ket          = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['keterangan']));
    
    // Hitung estimasi selesai
    $hari_dibutuhkan = (int) ceil($total_ayat / $target_ayat);
    $tgl_selesai = date('Y-m-d', strtotime($tgl_mulai . " + $hari_dibutuhkan days"));

    $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE target_hafalan SET 
            target_ayat_per_hari = $target_ayat, 
            target_surat = '$target_surat', 
            total_ayat_target = $total_ayat, 
            tanggal_mulai = '$tgl_mulai', 
            tanggal_estimasi_selesai = '$tgl_selesai', 
            keterangan = '$ket' 
            WHERE id_santri = $id_santri");
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO target_hafalan 
            (id_santri, target_ayat_per_hari, target_surat, total_ayat_target, tanggal_mulai, tanggal_estimasi_selesai, keterangan) 
            VALUES ($id_santri, $target_ayat, '$target_surat', $total_ayat, '$tgl_mulai', '$tgl_selesai', '$ket')");
    }
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Target hafalan santri berhasil disimpan!";
    header("Location: target_santri.php"); 
    exit;
}

// Hapus Target jika diminta
if (isset($_GET['hapus'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_GET['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM target_hafalan WHERE id = $id_hapus");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data target hafalan berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: target_santri.php");
    exit;
}

// Filter santri khusus binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah yang login
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $id_ustadz, $role, 's');

$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_binaan 
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Ambil daftar target khusus santri binaan beserta progres ziyadahnya
$sql_target = "SELECT t.*, s.nama, s.nis, s.jenis_kelamin, hl.nama_halaqah,
               COALESCE((
                   SELECT SUM(h.jumlah_ayat) 
                   FROM hafalan h 
                   WHERE h.id_santri = t.id_santri 
                     AND h.jenis = 'ziyadah' 
                     AND h.tanggal >= t.tanggal_mulai
               ), 0) AS ayat_tercapai
               FROM target_hafalan t 
               JOIN santri s ON t.id_santri = s.id 
               LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
               $where_binaan
               ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$target_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $sql_target), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
?>

===== pages/wali/monitoring.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'ortu') {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$anak_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($anak_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$santri = null;
foreach ($anak_list as $a) {
    if ($a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih) {
        $santri = $a;
        break;
    }
}

$target_data = null;
$ayat_tercapai_target = 0;
$stat_data = ['total_ziyadah' => 0, 'total_murajaah' => 0, 'total_tasmi' => 0];
$riwayat_list = [];
$chart_labels = [];
$chart_ziyadah = [];

if ($santri) {
    $id_santri = $santri['id'];
    $bulan_ini = date('Y-m');

    // Ambil Target Aktif & Progresnya
    $target_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"));
    if ($target_data) {
        $tgl_mulai = $target_data['tanggal_mulai'];
        $q_capai = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(jumlah_ayat), 0) as capai FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND jenis = 'ziyadah' AND tanggal >= '$tgl_mulai'");
        $ayat_tercapai_target = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_capai)['capai'];
    }

    // Statistik Bulan Ini
    $stat_query = "SELECT 
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ziyadah,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi
                   FROM hafalan 
                   WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'";
    $stat_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $stat_query));

    // Grafik 6 Bulan
    for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
        $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $q = "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as z FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'";
        $chart_ziyadah[] = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $q))['z'];
    }

    // Riwayat 20 Setoran Terakhir
    $riwayat_query = "SELECT h.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz 
                      FROM hafalan h 
                      LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id 
                      WHERE h.id_santri = $id_santri 
                      ORDER BY h.tanggal DESC, h.id DESC LIMIT 20";
    $riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
}
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Monitoring Progres Anak - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <?php if (!$santri): ?>
            <div class="bg-amber-50 border-l-4 border-amber-500 text-amber-800 p-6 rounded-2xl">
                <h3 class="font-bold text-lg mb-1">Data Anak Belum Terhubung</h3>
                <p class="text-sm">Akun orang tua Anda belum ditautkan dengan data santri. Silakan hubungi Ustadz pembimbing.</p>
            </div>
        <?php else: ?>

        <!-- Header -->
        <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-end gap-4 mb-8">
            <div>
                <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div class="p-2.5 bg-indigo-100 rounded-xl"><i data-lucide="book-open-check" class="w-7 h-7 text-indigo-600"></i></div>
                    Progres Hafalan Ananda
                </h1>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">Pantau capaian target, statistik bulanan, dan catatan evaluasi dari Ustadz pembimbing.</p>
            </div>

            <?php if (count($anak_list) > 1): ?>
            <form method="GET" class="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                <label class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">Pilih Ananda:</label>
                <select name="id_santri" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                    <?php foreach ($anak_list as $a): ?>
                        <option value="<?= $a['id'] ?>" <?= $a['id'] == $santri['id'] ? 'selected' : '' ?>><?= htmlspecialchars($a['nama']) ?></option>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                </select>
            </form>
            <?php endif; ?>
        </div>

        <!-- PROFIL & REALISASI TARGET -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-8">
            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4">
                <div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-extrabold text-2xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <?= strtoupper(substr($santri['nama'], 0, 1)) ?>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <span class="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">Santri Aktif</span>
                    <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= htmlspecialchars($santri['nama']) ?></h2>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">NIS: <?= htmlspecialchars($santri['nis']) ?> • <?= $santri['jenis_kelamin'] == 'L' ? 'Putra' : 'Putri' ?></p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="lg:col-span-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
                <?php if ($target_data):

                        <span>Tercapai: <b><?= $ayat_tercapai_target ?> Ayat</b></span>

                    <div class="h-full flex items-center justify-between">
                        <div>

===== pages/wali/pr.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya untuk Role Orang Tua (ortu)
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'ortu') { 
    header("Location: login.php"); 
    exit; 
}

$id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom status_ortu di tabel `pr` jika belum ada
$cek_kolom = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM pr LIKE 'status_ortu'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE pr ADD COLUMN status_ortu ENUM('Belum','Selesai') DEFAULT 'Belum' AFTER deskripsi");
}

// Ambil daftar anak yang terhubung dengan akun orang tua ini
$anak_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($anak_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$santri = null;
foreach ($anak_list as $a) {
    if ($a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih) {
        $santri = $a;
        break;
    }
}

// Proses Toggle Status PR ("Sudah Disimak di Rumah" / "Belum")
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['toggle_status_pr']) && $santri) {
    $id_pr = (int) $_POST['id_pr'];
    $status_baru = ($_POST['status_sekarang'] === 'Selesai') ? 'Belum' : 'Selesai';
    
    mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE pr SET status_ortu = '$status_baru' WHERE id = $id_pr AND id_santri = {$santri['id']}");
    $_SESSION['success'] = ($status_baru === 'Selesai') 
        ? "Alhamdulillah, tugas rumah ditandai sudah disimak di rumah!" 
        : "Status tugas rumah dikembalikan menjadi belum disimak.";
    
    header("Location: pr_anak.php?id_santri=" . $santri['id']);
    exit;
}

$pr_list = [];
$total_pr = 0;
$pr_selesai = 0;
$pr_belum = 0;

if ($santri) {
    $q = "SELECT p.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz 
          FROM pr p 
          LEFT JOIN users u ON p.id_ustadz = u.id 
          WHERE p.id_santri = {$santri['id']} 
          ORDER BY FIELD(p.status_ortu, 'Belum', 'Selesai'), p.tanggal DESC, p.id DESC";
    $pr_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $q), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

    $total_pr = count($pr_list);
    foreach ($pr_list as $item) {
        if (($item['status_ortu'] ?? 'Belum') === 'Selesai') {
            $pr_selesai++;
        } else {
            $pr_belum++;
        }
    }
}

$persen_selesai = $total_pr > 0 ? round(($pr_selesai / $total_pr) * 100) : 0;

$Nama_Hari_Indo = [
    'Sunday' => 'Minggu', 'Monday' => 'Senin', 'Tuesday' => 'Selasa',
    'Wednesday' => 'Rabu', 'Thursday' => 'Kamis', 'Friday' => 'Jumat', 'Saturday' => 'Sabtu'
];
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Tugas Rumah (PR) Ananda - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <!-- Header Halaman -->
        <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-end gap-4 mb-8">
            <div>
                <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div class="p-2.5 bg-orange-100 rounded-xl">
                        <i data-lucide="clipboard-list" class="w-7 h-7 text-orange-600"></i>
                    </div>
                    Tugas Rumah (PR) Ananda
                </h1>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">Dampingi ananda menyelesaikan tugas hafalan atau muraja'ah mandiri di rumah.</p>
            </div>

            <?php if (count($anak_list) > 1): ?>
            <form method="GET" action="pr_anak.php" class="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                <label class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">Pilih Ananda:</label>
                <select name="id_santri" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                    <?php foreach ($anak_list as $a): ?>
                        <option value="<?= $a['id'] ?>" <?= $a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih ? 'selected' : '' ?>>
                            <?= htmlspecialchars($a['nama']) ?>
                        </option>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                </select>
            </form>
            <?php endif; ?>
        </div>

        <!-- Notifikasi Sukses -->
        <?php if (isset($_SESSION['success'])): ?>
            <div class="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl mb-6 text-sm flex items-center gap-3 shadow-sm">
                <i data-lucide="check-circle-2" class="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0"></i>
                <span class="font-medium"><?= $_SESSION['success']; unset($_SESSION['success']); ?></span>
            </div>
        <?php endif; ?>

        <?php if (!$santri): ?>
            <div class="bg-amber-50 border-l-4 border-amber-500 text-amber-800 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm text-sm">
                Data santri belum terhubung dengan akun orang tua Anda. Silakan hubungi Ustadz/Ustadzah pembimbing.
            </div>
        <?php else: ?>

        <!-- RINGKASAN PROFIL & STATISTIK PR -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5 mb-8">
            
            <!-- Kartu Profil & Progress -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-4">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                        <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-orange-500 to-amber-600 text-white font-extrabold text-lg flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm">
                            <?= strtoupper(substr($santri['nama'], 0, 1)) ?>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 class="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900"><?= htmlspecialchars($santri['nama']) ?></h2>
                            <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">NIS: <?= htmlspecialchars($santri['nis'] ?: '-') ?> • Progres Pendampingan Rumah</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <span class="text-xl font-black text-orange-600 bg-orange-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl border border-orange-200">
                        <?= $persen_selesai ?>%
                    </span>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <div class="w-full h-2.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 mb-1.5">
                        <div class="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-emerald-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-500" style="width: <?= $persen_selesai ?>%"></div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-medium">
                        <span>Disimak Ortu: <b class="text-emerald-600"><?= $pr_selesai ?> Tugas</b></span>
                        <span>Total Tugas: <b class="text-slate-700"><?= $total_pr ?> Tugas</b></span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Belum Disimak -->
            <div class="bg-white border border-amber-200 border-b-4 border-b-amber-500 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-amber-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Perlu Disimak</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $pr_belum ?></p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Tugas aktif di rumah</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="clock" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Sudah Disimak -->
            <div class="bg-white border border-emerald-200 border-b-4 border-b-emerald-500 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Sudah Disimak</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $pr_selesai ?></p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Selesai didampingi</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="check-circle-2" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>

        <!-- TOOLBAR FILTER & PENCARIAN -->
        <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-4 mb-6 shadow-sm flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button type="button" onclick="setFilterPR('ALL', this)" class="tab-filter bg-slate-900 text-white text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition">
                    Semua Tugas (<?= $total_pr ?>)
                </button>
                <button type="button" onclick="setFilterPR('Belum', this)" class="tab-filter bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition">
                    ⏳ Belum Disimak (<?= $pr_belum ?>)
                </button>
                <button type="button" onclick="setFilterPR('Selesai', this)" class="tab-filter bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition">
                    ✅ Sudah Disimak (<?= $pr_selesai ?>)
                </button>
            </div>

            <div class="relative w-full sm:w-64">
                <i data-lucide="search" class="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5"></i>
                <input type="text" id="cariPR" oninput="terapkanFilterPR()" placeholder="Cari judul atau ayat PR..." 
                    class="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-500 outline-none">
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- DAFTAR KARTU PR -->
        <?php if (empty($pr_list)): ?>
            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-12 text-center shadow-sm">
                <div class="w-16 h-16 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
                    <i data-lucide="sparkles" class="w-8 h-8"></i>
                </div>
                <h3 class="text-base font-bold text-slate-800">Belum Ada Tugas Rumah</h3>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Saat ini belum ada PR yang diberikan oleh Ustadz/Ustadzah pembimbing.</p>
            </div>
        <?php else: ?>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6" id="gridPR">
                <?php foreach ($pr_list as $p): 
                    $status = $p['status_ortu'] ?? 'Belum';
                    $is_selesai = ($status === 'Selesai');
                    $is_baru = (!$is_selesai && strtotime($p['tanggal']) >= strtotime('-2 days'));
                    
                    $hari_en = date('l', strtotime($p['tanggal']));
                    $hari_indo = $Nama_Hari_Indo[$hari_en] ?? $hari_en;
                ?>
                <div class="kartu-pr bg-white border <?= $is_selesai ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/10' : ($is_baru ? 'border-orange-300 ring-2 ring-orange-100' : 'border-slate-200') ?> rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between transition hover:shadow-md"
                     data-status="<?= $status ?>"
                     data-search="<?= strtolower(htmlspecialchars($p['judul'] . ' ' . $p['deskripsi'])) ?>">

                                    </span>

                            <?= htmlspecialchars($p['judul']) ?>

                        <div class="p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 rounded-xl text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed mb-5">
